439:             kind: UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::RegionReservation,
440:             process: None,
441:             frame: None,
442:         });
443:     }
444:     let process = match roots.create(allocator) {
445:         Ok(process) => process,
446:         Err(failure) => {
447:             let frame = failure.frame;
448:             let frame = frame.and_then(|frame| match allocator.deallocate(frame) {
449:                 Ok(()) => None,
450:                 Err(error) => Some(error.frame),
451:             });
452:             return Err(UserProgramPrepareError {
453:                 kind: UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::RootCreation,
454:                 process: None,
455:                 frame,
456:             });
457:         }
458:     };
459:     let mut regions = UserRegionTable::new();
460:     let stack_layout = match regions.reserve_elf_layout(
461:         &image,
462:         stack_base,
463:         stack_pages,
464:         guard_pages,
465:         virtual_address_bits,
466:     ) {
467:         Ok(layout) => layout,
468:         Err(_) => {
469:             return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
470:                 roots,
471:                 allocator,
472:                 process,
473:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::RegionReservation,
474:                 None,
475:             ));
476:         }
477:     };
478:     if let Err(failure) =
479:         image.load_into_process_root(roots, allocator, process, user_memory, &mut *select_cache)
480:     {
481:         return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
482:             roots,
483:             allocator,
484:             process,
485:             UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::ElfLoad,
486:             failure.frame,
487:         ));
488:     }
489: 
490:     let mut guard_probe = [0u8; 1];
491:     // SAFETY: The scratch byte is kernel stack storage, disjoint from user frames.
492:     // The reserved guard range must have no mapped user backing.
493:     match unsafe {
494:         roots.copy_from_user(
495:             process,
496:             stack_layout.guard_region().start(),
497:             &mut guard_probe,
498:             user_memory,
499:         )
500:     } {
501:         Err(failure) if matches!(failure.cause, crate::paging::ProcessUserCopyError::Unmapped) => {}
502:         _ => {
503:             return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
504:                 roots,
505:                 allocator,
506:                 process,
507:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackGuard,
508:                 None,
509:             ));
510:         }
511:     }
512: 
513:     for offset in (0..stack_layout.stack_region().length()).step_by(PAGE_SIZE as usize) {
514:         let frame = match allocator.allocate() {
515:             Some(frame) => frame,
516:             None => {
517:                 return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
518:                     roots,
519:                     allocator,
520:                     process,
521:                     UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackAllocation,
522:                     None,
523:                 ));
524:             }
525:         };
526:         if crate::paging::zero_user_frame(&frame, user_memory).is_err() {
527:             return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
528:                 roots,
529:                 allocator,
530:                 process,
531:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackInitialization,
532:                 Some(frame),
533:             ));
534:         }
535:         let Some(virtual_address) = stack_layout.stack_region().start().checked_add(offset) else {
536:             return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
537:                 roots,
538:                 allocator,
539:                 process,
540:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackMapping,
541:                 Some(frame),
542:             ));
543:         };
544:         let mapping = match validate_mapping_range(
545:             virtual_address,
546:             frame.address(),
547:             PAGE_SIZE,
548:             PagePermissions {
549:                 writable: true,
550:                 executable: false,
551:                 user_accessible: true,
552:             },
553:             Some(physical_address_bits),
554:             virtual_address_bits,
555:         ) {
556:             Ok(mapping) => mapping,
557:             Err(_) => {
558:                 return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
559:                     roots,
560:                     allocator,
561:                     process,
562:                     UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackMapping,
563:                     Some(frame),
564:                 ));
565:             }
566:         };
567:         let cache_attributes = match select_cache(mapping) {
568:             Ok(attributes) => attributes,
569:             Err(message) => {
570:                 return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
571:                     roots,
572:                     allocator,
573:                     process,
574:                     UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::CachePolicy(message),
575:                     Some(frame),
576:                 ));
577:             }
578:         };
579:         if let Err(failure) =
580:             roots.map_user_page(allocator, process, frame, mapping, cache_attributes)
581:         {
582:             return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
583:                 roots,
584:                 allocator,
585:                 process,
586:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackMapping,
587:                 Some(failure.frame),
588:             ));
589:         }
590:     }
591: 
592:     if roots
593:         .validate_initial_user_state(process, image.entry_point(), stack_layout.stack_pointer())
594:         .is_err()
595:     {
596:         return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
597:             roots,
598:             allocator,
599:             process,
600:             UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::InvalidInitialState,
601:             None,
602:         ));
603:     }
604:     let raw_frame = crate::syscall::UserIretFrame {
605:         instruction_pointer: image.entry_point(),
606:         code_selector: u64::from(user_code_selector),
607:         flags: crate::syscall::SAFE_USER_RFLAGS,
608:         stack_pointer: stack_layout.stack_pointer(),
609:         stack_selector: u64::from(user_data_selector),
610:     };
611:     let user_frame = match crate::syscall::sanitize_user_iret_frame(raw_frame, virtual_address_bits)
612:     {
613:         Ok(frame) => frame,
614:         Err(_) => {
615:             return Err(cleanup_failed_user_program(
616:                 roots,
617:                 allocator,
618:                 process,
619:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::InvalidIretFrame,
620:                 None,
621:             ));
622:         }
623:     };
624: 
625:     Ok(PreparedUserProgram {
626:         process,
627:         regions,
628:         user_frame,
629:     })
630: }
631: 
632: fn validate_user_region_range(
633:     start: u64,
634:     length: u64,
635:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
636: ) -> Result<(), UserRegionError> {
637:     if !matches!(virtual_address_bits, 48 | 57) {
638:         return Err(UserRegionError::InvalidAddressWidth);
639:     }
640:     if length == 0 {
641:         return Err(UserRegionError::EmptyRange);
642:     }
643:     let page_size = crate::paging::PAGE_SIZE;
644:     if (start | length) & (page_size - 1) != 0 {
645:         return Err(UserRegionError::UnalignedRange);
646:     }
647:     let end = start
648:         .checked_add(length)
649:         .ok_or(UserRegionError::AddressOverflow)?;
650:     let lower_half_end = 1u64 << (virtual_address_bits - 1);
651:     if start >= lower_half_end
652:         || end > lower_half_end
653:         || !crate::memory::is_canonical_virtual_address(start, virtual_address_bits)
654:         || !crate::memory::is_canonical_virtual_address(end - 1, virtual_address_bits)
655:     {
656:         return Err(UserRegionError::NonCanonicalRange);
657:     }
658:     Ok(())
659: }
660: 
661: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
662: pub enum ProcessState {
663:     Running,
664:     Exited(i32),
665: }
666: 
667: /// A nonblocking observation of a child. `try_wait` reaps an exited child; `peek_wait` does not.
668: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
669: pub enum WaitResult {
670:     Running { pid: ProcessId },
671:     Exited { pid: ProcessId, status: i32 },
672: }
673: 
674: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
675: pub enum ProcessTableError {
676:     Capacity,
677:     InitialProcessAlreadyExists,
678:     InvalidProcess,
679:     InvalidParent,
680:     ParentExited,
681:     AlreadyExited,
682:     NoChild,
683:     ChildStillRunning,
684:     BadFileDescriptor,
685:     DescriptorTableFull,
686:     ProcessIdExhausted,
687: }
688: 
689: #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
690: struct ProcessRecord {
691:     pid: ProcessId,
692:     parent: ProcessId,
693:     state: ProcessState,
694:     descriptors: [Option<crate::syscall::ConsoleStream>; MAX_PROCESS_DESCRIPTORS],
695: }
696: 
697: fn initial_console_descriptors() -> [Option<crate::syscall::ConsoleStream>; MAX_PROCESS_DESCRIPTORS]
698: {
699:     let mut descriptors = [None; MAX_PROCESS_DESCRIPTORS];
700:     descriptors[1] = Some(crate::syscall::ConsoleStream::Stdout);
701:     descriptors[2] = Some(crate::syscall::ConsoleStream::Stderr);
702:     descriptors
703: }
704: 
705: /// Bounded, allocation-free process lifecycle records for the first user slice.
706: ///
707: /// Exited records remain waitable zombies until their parent calls `try_wait`.
708: /// If a parent exits first, its children are reparented to the kernel supervisor
709: /// so their exit records remain reapable. This table models only inherited console
710: /// stream descriptors; file objects and address-space ownership live elsewhere.
711: pub struct ProcessTable {
712:     records: [Option<ProcessRecord>; MAX_PROCESSES],
713:     next_pid: Option<u64>,
714: }
715: 
716: impl ProcessTable {
717:     pub const fn new() -> Self {
718:         Self {
719:             records: [None; MAX_PROCESSES],
720:             next_pid: Some(1),
721:         }
722:     }
723: 
724:     /// Register the one boot-created process under the kernel supervisor.
725:     pub fn create_initial(&mut self) -> Result<ProcessId, ProcessTableError> {
726:         if self
727:             .records
728:             .iter()
729:             .flatten()
730:             .any(|record| record.parent == KERNEL_PROCESS_ID)
731:         {
732:             return Err(ProcessTableError::InitialProcessAlreadyExists);
733:         }
734:         self.insert(KERNEL_PROCESS_ID, initial_console_descriptors())
735:     }
736: 
737:     /// Register a child of a currently running process.
738:     pub fn spawn(&mut self, parent: ProcessId) -> Result<ProcessId, ProcessTableError> {
739:         let parent_record = self
740:             .record(parent)
741:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::InvalidParent)?;
742:         if parent_record.state != ProcessState::Running {
743:             return Err(ProcessTableError::ParentExited);
744:         }
745:         self.insert(parent, parent_record.descriptors)
746:     }
747: 
748:     /// Record an exit status without reclaiming the process identity.
749:     pub fn exit(&mut self, pid: ProcessId, status: i32) -> Result<(), ProcessTableError> {
750:         let index = self
751:             .records
752:             .iter()
753:             .position(|record| record.is_some_and(|record| record.pid == pid))
754:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::InvalidProcess)?;
755:         let record = self.records[index]
756:             .as_mut()
757:             .expect("process slot was found");
758:         if record.state != ProcessState::Running {
759:             return Err(ProcessTableError::AlreadyExited);
760:         }
761:         record.state = ProcessState::Exited(status);
762:         record.descriptors = [None; MAX_PROCESS_DESCRIPTORS];
763:         for child in self.records.iter_mut().flatten() {
764:             if child.parent == pid {
765:                 child.parent = KERNEL_PROCESS_ID;
766:             }
767:         }
768:         Ok(())
769:     }
770: 
771:     /// Inspect a matching child without consuming an exited record.
772:     ///
773:     /// `child_pid == 0` selects any child, preferring the lowest-PID exited child.
774:     /// This split lets callers validate/copy exit status before reaping a zombie.
775:     pub fn peek_wait(
776:         &self,
777:         parent: ProcessId,
778:         child_pid: u64,
779:     ) -> Result<WaitResult, ProcessTableError> {
780:         if parent != KERNEL_PROCESS_ID {
781:             let parent_record = self
782:                 .record(parent)
783:                 .ok_or(ProcessTableError::InvalidParent)?;
784:             if parent_record.state != ProcessState::Running {
785:                 return Err(ProcessTableError::ParentExited);
786:             }
787:         }
788: 
789:         let mut found_child = false;
790:         let mut running: Option<ProcessId> = None;
791:         let mut exited: Option<(ProcessId, i32)> = None;
792:         for record in self.records.iter().flatten().filter(|record| {
793:             record.parent == parent && (child_pid == 0 || record.pid.get() == child_pid)
794:         }) {
795:             found_child = true;
796:             match record.state {
797:                 ProcessState::Running => {
798:                     if running.is_none_or(|pid| record.pid < pid) {
799:                         running = Some(record.pid);
800:                     }
801:                 }
802:                 ProcessState::Exited(status) => {
803:                     if exited.is_none_or(|(pid, _)| record.pid < pid) {
804:                         exited = Some((record.pid, status));
805:                     }
806:                 }
807:             }
808:         }
809: 
810:         if let Some((pid, status)) = exited {
811:             return Ok(WaitResult::Exited { pid, status });
812:         }
813:         if let Some(pid) = running {
814:             return Ok(WaitResult::Running { pid });
815:         }
816:         if found_child {
817:             unreachable!("a matching child must be running or exited");
818:         }
819:         Err(ProcessTableError::NoChild)
820:     }
821: 
822:     /// Reap one specific exited child after its status has been consumed.
823:     pub fn reap_exited(
824:         &mut self,
825:         parent: ProcessId,
826:         child_pid: ProcessId,
827:     ) -> Result<WaitResult, ProcessTableError> {
828:         let result = self.peek_wait(parent, child_pid.get())?;
829:         let WaitResult::Exited { pid, status } = result else {
830:             return Err(ProcessTableError::ChildStillRunning);
831:         };
832:         let index = self
833:             .records
834:             .iter()
835:             .position(|record| {
836:                 record.is_some_and(|record| record.pid == pid && record.parent == parent)
837:             })
838:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::NoChild)?;
839:         self.records[index] = None;
840:         Ok(WaitResult::Exited { pid, status })
841:     }
842: 
843:     /// Inspect or reap a matching child without blocking.
844:     ///
845:     /// `child_pid == 0` selects any child, preferring the lowest-PID exited child.
846:     pub fn try_wait(
847:         &mut self,
848:         parent: ProcessId,
849:         child_pid: u64,
850:     ) -> Result<WaitResult, ProcessTableError> {
851:         let result = self.peek_wait(parent, child_pid)?;
852:         if let WaitResult::Exited { pid, .. } = result {
853:             let _ = self.reap_exited(parent, pid)?;
854:         }
855:         Ok(result)
856:     }
857: 
858:     pub fn state(&self, pid: ProcessId) -> Option<ProcessState> {
859:         self.record(pid).map(|record| record.state)
860:     }
861: 
862:     pub fn live_records(&self) -> usize {
863:         self.records.iter().flatten().count()
864:     }
865: 
866:     /// Resolve one live descriptor to its bounded console-stream target.
867:     pub fn descriptor_stream(
868:         &self,
869:         pid: ProcessId,
870:         descriptor: u64,
871:     ) -> Option<crate::syscall::ConsoleStream> {
872:         let descriptor = usize::try_from(descriptor).ok()?;
873:         self.record(pid)?
874:             .descriptors
875:             .get(descriptor)
876:             .copied()
877:             .flatten()
878:     }
879: 
880:     /// Close one descriptor without affecting duplicated descriptors to the same stream.
881:     pub fn close_descriptor(
882:         &mut self,
883:         pid: ProcessId,
884:         descriptor: u64,
885:     ) -> Result<(), ProcessTableError> {
886:         let descriptor =
887:             usize::try_from(descriptor).map_err(|_| ProcessTableError::BadFileDescriptor)?;
888:         let record = self
889:             .records
890:             .iter_mut()
891:             .flatten()
892:             .find(|record| record.pid == pid)
893:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::InvalidProcess)?;
894:         if record.state != ProcessState::Running {
895:             return Err(ProcessTableError::AlreadyExited);
896:         }
897:         let entry = record
898:             .descriptors
899:             .get_mut(descriptor)
900:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::BadFileDescriptor)?;
901:         entry.take().ok_or(ProcessTableError::BadFileDescriptor)?;
902:         Ok(())
903:     }
904: 
905:     /// Duplicate a descriptor into the lowest free slot at or above descriptor 3.
906:     pub fn duplicate_descriptor(
907:         &mut self,
908:         pid: ProcessId,
909:         descriptor: u64,
910:     ) -> Result<u64, ProcessTableError> {
911:         let source = self
912:             .descriptor_stream(pid, descriptor)
913:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::BadFileDescriptor)?;
914:         let record = self
915:             .records
916:             .iter_mut()
917:             .flatten()
918:             .find(|record| record.pid == pid)
919:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::InvalidProcess)?;
920:         if record.state != ProcessState::Running {
921:             return Err(ProcessTableError::AlreadyExited);
922:         }
923:         let slot = record
924:             .descriptors
925:             .iter()
926:             .enumerate()
927:             .skip(3)
928:             .find_map(|(index, descriptor)| descriptor.is_none().then_some(index))
929:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::DescriptorTableFull)?;
930:         record.descriptors[slot] = Some(source);
931:         Ok(slot as u64)
932:     }
933: 
934:     fn insert(
935:         &mut self,
936:         parent: ProcessId,
937:         descriptors: [Option<crate::syscall::ConsoleStream>; MAX_PROCESS_DESCRIPTORS],
938:     ) -> Result<ProcessId, ProcessTableError> {
939:         let slot = self
940:             .records
941:             .iter()
942:             .position(Option::is_none)
943:             .ok_or(ProcessTableError::Capacity)?;
944:         let pid = self.next_pid.ok_or(ProcessTableError::ProcessIdExhausted)?;
945:         self.next_pid = pid.checked_add(1);
946:         let pid = ProcessId(pid);
947:         self.records[slot] = Some(ProcessRecord {
948:             pid,
949:             parent,
950:             state: ProcessState::Running,
951:             descriptors,
952:         });
953:         Ok(pid)
954:     }
955: 
956:     fn record(&self, pid: ProcessId) -> Option<&ProcessRecord> {
957:         self.records
958:             .iter()
959:             .flatten()
960:             .find(|record| record.pid == pid)
961:     }
962: }
963: 
964: impl Default for ProcessTable {
965:     fn default() -> Self {
966:         Self::new()
967:     }
968: }
969: 
970: #[cfg(test)]
971: mod tests {
972:     use super::{
973:         ProcessState, ProcessTable, ProcessTableError, UserRegionError, UserRegionKind,
974:         UserRegionTable, WaitResult, KERNEL_PROCESS_ID, MAX_PROCESSES, MAX_PROCESS_DESCRIPTORS,
975:         USER_ANONYMOUS_MAP_START,
976:     };
977: 
978:     #[test]
979:     fn child_exit_status_is_retained_until_exact_wait_reaps_it() {
980:         let mut processes = ProcessTable::new();
981:         let init = processes.create_initial().unwrap();
982:         let child = processes.spawn(init).unwrap();
983: 
984:         assert_eq!(
985:             processes.try_wait(init, child.get()),
986:             Ok(WaitResult::Running { pid: child })
987:         );
988:         processes.exit(child, -17).unwrap();
989:         assert_eq!(processes.state(child), Some(ProcessState::Exited(-17)));
990:         assert_eq!(
991:             processes.try_wait(init, child.get()),
992:             Ok(WaitResult::Exited {
993:                 pid: child,
994:                 status: -17,
995:             })
996:         );
997:         assert_eq!(processes.state(child), None);
998:         assert_eq!(
999:             processes.try_wait(init, child.get()),
1000:             Err(ProcessTableError::NoChild)
1001:         );
1002:     }
1003: 
1004:     #[test]
1005:     fn peek_wait_keeps_a_zombie_until_status_consumption_succeeds() {
1006:         let mut processes = ProcessTable::new();
1007:         let parent = processes.create_initial().unwrap();
1008:         let child = processes.spawn(parent).unwrap();
1009:         processes.exit(child, 41).unwrap();
1010: 
1011:         assert_eq!(
1012:             processes.peek_wait(parent, child.get()),
1013:             Ok(WaitResult::Exited {
1014:                 pid: child,
1015:                 status: 41,
1016:             })
1017:         );
1018:         assert_eq!(processes.state(child), Some(ProcessState::Exited(41)));
1019:         assert_eq!(
1020:             processes.reap_exited(parent, child),
1021:             Ok(WaitResult::Exited {
1022:                 pid: child,
1023:                 status: 41,
1024:             })
1025:         );
1026:         assert_eq!(processes.state(child), None);
1027:         assert_eq!(
1028:             processes.reap_exited(parent, child),
1029:             Err(ProcessTableError::NoChild)
1030:         );
1031:     }
1032: 
1033:     #[test]
1034:     fn reaping_a_running_child_is_rejected_without_mutation() {
1035:         let mut processes = ProcessTable::new();
1036:         let parent = processes.create_initial().unwrap();
1037:         let child = processes.spawn(parent).unwrap();
1038: 
1039:         assert_eq!(
1040:             processes.reap_exited(parent, child),
1041:             Err(ProcessTableError::ChildStillRunning)
1042:         );
1043:         assert_eq!(processes.state(child), Some(ProcessState::Running));
1044:     }
1045: 
1046:     #[test]
1047:     fn wait_any_prefers_the_lowest_pid_zombie_and_keeps_running_children() {
1048:         let mut processes = ProcessTable::new();
1049:         let init = processes.create_initial().unwrap();
1050:         let first = processes.spawn(init).unwrap();
1051:         let second = processes.spawn(init).unwrap();
1052:         processes.exit(second, 23).unwrap();
1053: 
1054:         assert_eq!(
1055:             processes.try_wait(init, 0),
1056:             Ok(WaitResult::Exited {
1057:                 pid: second,
1058:                 status: 23,
1059:             })
1060:         );
1061:         assert_eq!(
1062:             processes.try_wait(init, 0),
1063:             Ok(WaitResult::Running { pid: first })
1064:         );
1065:     }
1066: 
1067:     #[test]
1068:     fn exiting_parent_reparents_children_to_kernel_supervisor() {
1069:         let mut processes = ProcessTable::new();
1070:         let init = processes.create_initial().unwrap();
1071:         let child = processes.spawn(init).unwrap();
1072:         processes.exit(init, 0).unwrap();
1073: 
1074:         assert_eq!(
1075:             processes.try_wait(KERNEL_PROCESS_ID, 0),
1076:             Ok(WaitResult::Exited {
1077:                 pid: init,
1078:                 status: 0,
1079:             })
1080:         );
1081:         processes.exit(child, 7).unwrap();
1082:         assert_eq!(
1083:             processes.try_wait(KERNEL_PROCESS_ID, child.get()),
1084:             Ok(WaitResult::Exited {
1085:                 pid: child,
1086:                 status: 7,
1087:             })
1088:         );
1089:     }
1090: 
1091:     #[test]
1092:     fn capacity_and_invalid_lifecycle_operations_are_explicit() {
1093:         let mut processes = ProcessTable::new();
1094:         assert_eq!(
1095:             processes.spawn(KERNEL_PROCESS_ID),
1096:             Err(ProcessTableError::InvalidParent)
1097:         );
1098:         let init = processes.create_initial().unwrap();
1099:         assert_eq!(
1100:             processes.create_initial(),
1101:             Err(ProcessTableError::InitialProcessAlreadyExists)
1102:         );
1103:         let children: [Option<super::ProcessId>; MAX_PROCESSES - 1] =
1104:             core::array::from_fn(|_| None);
1105:         let mut children = children;
1106:         for child in &mut children {
1107:             *child = Some(processes.spawn(init).unwrap());

136:     InvalidFlags,
137:     InvalidInstructionPointer,
138:     InvalidStackPointer,
139: }

141: /// Validate the initial privilege-return frame, including the required 16-byte
142: /// stack alignment, and replace user flags with a conservative kernel value.
143: ///
144: /// The safe flags deliberately leave IF clear until a process-aware interrupt
145: /// scheduler is installed. The caller must separately verify that RIP names an
146: /// executable process mapping and RSP names a writable stack mapping.
147: pub fn sanitize_user_iret_frame(
148:     frame: UserIretFrame,
149:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
150: ) -> Result<ValidatedUserIretFrame, UserIretFrameError> {
151:     sanitize_user_frame(frame, virtual_address_bits, true)
152: }
153: 
154: /// Sanitize a syscall's saved user return frame without imposing a new stack
155: /// alignment on an in-function RSP. SysV permits transient stack alignment while
156: /// executing a function; the active process root must still validate its mapping.
157: pub fn sanitize_user_syscall_return_frame(
158:     frame: UserIretFrame,
159:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
160: ) -> Result<ValidatedUserIretFrame, UserIretFrameError> {
161:     sanitize_user_frame(frame, virtual_address_bits, false)
162: }
163: 
164: fn sanitize_user_frame(
165:     frame: UserIretFrame,
166:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
167:     require_aligned_stack: bool,
168: ) -> Result<ValidatedUserIretFrame, UserIretFrameError> {
169:     if !matches!(virtual_address_bits, 48 | 57) {
170:         return Err(UserIretFrameError::InvalidAddressWidth);
171:     }
172:     if frame.code_selector != u64::from(USER_CODE_SELECTOR)
173:         || frame.stack_selector != u64::from(USER_DATA_SELECTOR)
174:     {
175:         return Err(UserIretFrameError::InvalidSelectors);
176:     }
177:     if frame.flags & (1 << 1) == 0
178:         || frame.flags & (RFLAGS_IOPL | RFLAGS_NESTED_TASK | RFLAGS_VIRTUAL_8086) != 0
179:     {
180:         return Err(UserIretFrameError::InvalidFlags);
181:     }
182: 
183:     let lower_half_end = 1u64 << (virtual_address_bits - 1);
184:     if frame.instruction_pointer >= lower_half_end
185:         || !crate::memory::is_canonical_virtual_address(
186:             frame.instruction_pointer,
187:             virtual_address_bits,
188:         )
189:     {
190:         return Err(UserIretFrameError::InvalidInstructionPointer);
191:     }
192:     let stack_last = frame
193:         .stack_pointer
194:         .checked_sub(1)
195:         .filter(|address| *address < lower_half_end)
196:         .filter(|address| {
197:             crate::memory::is_canonical_virtual_address(*address, virtual_address_bits)
198:         });
199:     if stack_last.is_none() || (require_aligned_stack && frame.stack_pointer & 0xf != 0) {
200:         return Err(UserIretFrameError::InvalidStackPointer);
201:     }
202: 
203:     Ok(ValidatedUserIretFrame(UserIretFrame {
204:         instruction_pointer: frame.instruction_pointer,
205:         code_selector: u64::from(USER_CODE_SELECTOR),
206:         flags: SAFE_USER_RFLAGS,
207:         stack_pointer: frame.stack_pointer,
208:         stack_selector: u64::from(USER_DATA_SELECTOR),
209:     }))
210: }

260: /// The kernel-side sink for the v1 console descriptors.
261: pub trait ConsoleOutput {
262:     type Error;
263: 
264:     fn write(&mut self, stream: ConsoleStream, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, Self::Error>;
265: }
266: 
267: /// Copy and write one bounded v1 user buffer through a process root.
268: ///
269: /// The complete user range is preflighted by `copy_from_user` before output.
270: /// If the frame backend faults after copying a prefix, that prefix is still
271: /// offered to the console and a positive short write is returned when accepted.
272: ///
273: /// # Safety
274: /// The current kernel stack, including this function's fixed scratch buffer,
275: /// must not be mapped as a user frame in `process`. The boot allocator enforces
276: /// this by excluding the kernel image and bootstrap-stack spans from usable
277: /// frame allocation; future kernel stacks must preserve the same invariant.
278: pub unsafe fn write_user_buffer<M, U, O>(
279:     roots: &crate::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
280:     process: crate::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceHandle,
281:     fd: u64,
282:     user_buffer: u64,
283:     length: usize,
284:     user_memory: &mut U,
285:     output: &mut O,
286: ) -> Result<usize, SyscallError>
287: where
288:     M: crate::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory,
289:     U: crate::paging::UserFrameMemory,
290:     O: ConsoleOutput,
291: {
292:     let stream = match fd {
293:         1 => ConsoleStream::Stdout,
294:         2 => ConsoleStream::Stderr,
295:         _ => return Err(SyscallError::BadFileDescriptor),
296:     };
297:     // SAFETY: The caller's stack/user-frame disjointness contract applies to the
298:     // shared stream-based copy-and-write implementation below.
299:     unsafe {
300:         write_user_buffer_to_stream(
301:             roots,
302:             process,
303:             stream,
304:             user_buffer,
305:             length,
306:             user_memory,
307:             output,
308:         )
309:     }
310: }
311: 
312: /// Copy and write one bounded user buffer to an already-authorized console stream.
313: ///
314: /// # Safety
315: /// The current kernel stack, including the fixed scratch buffer, must not be
316: /// mapped as a user frame in `process`. The caller must authorize `stream` for
317: /// this process before calling; this function does not validate descriptor state.
318: pub unsafe fn write_user_buffer_to_stream<M, U, O>(
319:     roots: &crate::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
320:     process: crate::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceHandle,
321:     stream: ConsoleStream,
322:     user_buffer: u64,
323:     length: usize,
324:     user_memory: &mut U,
325:     output: &mut O,
326: ) -> Result<usize, SyscallError>
327: where
328:     M: crate::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory,
329:     U: crate::paging::UserFrameMemory,
330:     O: ConsoleOutput,
331: {
332:     use crate::paging::{ProcessUserCopyError, MAX_PROCESS_USER_COPY_BYTES};
333: 
334:     if length > MAX_WRITE_BYTES as usize {
335:         return Err(SyscallError::TooBig);
336:     }
337:     if length == 0 {
338:         return Ok(0);
339:     }
340: 
341:     let mut scratch = [0; MAX_PROCESS_USER_COPY_BYTES];
342:     let copied = match unsafe {
343:         roots.copy_from_user(process, user_buffer, &mut scratch[..length], user_memory)
344:     } {
345:         Ok(copied) if copied <= length => copied,
346:         Ok(_) => return Err(SyscallError::Fault),
347:         Err(failure) if failure.transferred > 0 && failure.transferred <= length => {
348:             failure.transferred
349:         }
350:         Err(failure) => {
351:             return Err(match failure.cause {
352:                 ProcessUserCopyError::LengthTooLarge => SyscallError::TooBig,
353:                 ProcessUserCopyError::Memory(_) => SyscallError::Io,
354:                 ProcessUserCopyError::InvalidHandle
355:                 | ProcessUserCopyError::Releasing
356:                 | ProcessUserCopyError::InvalidRange
357:                 | ProcessUserCopyError::Unmapped
358:                 | ProcessUserCopyError::PermissionDenied => SyscallError::Fault,
359:             });
360:         }
361:     };
362:     if copied == 0 {
363:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
364:     }
365: 
366:     let written = output
367:         .write(stream, &scratch[..copied])
368:         .map_err(|_| SyscallError::Io)?;
369:     if written == 0 || written > copied {
370:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
371:     }
372:     Ok(written)
373: }
374: 
375: /// Decode a v1 syscall number and its register arguments without touching user memory.
376: ///
377: /// The exit status uses the low 32 bits of RDI, interpreted as a signed value.
378: /// A zero-length write preserves the supplied pointer without validating it; the
379: /// eventual syscall implementation must return zero before inspecting that pointer.
380: pub const fn decode_syscall(
381:     syscall_number: u64,
382:     arguments: SyscallArguments,
383: ) -> Result<SyscallRequest, SyscallError> {
384:     match syscall_number {
385:         number::ABI_VERSION => Ok(SyscallRequest::AbiVersion),
386:         number::EXIT => Ok(SyscallRequest::Exit {
387:             status: arguments.rdi as u32 as i32,
388:         }),
389:         number::WRITE => {
390:             if arguments.rdx > MAX_WRITE_BYTES {
391:                 return Err(SyscallError::TooBig);
392:             }
393:             Ok(SyscallRequest::Write {
394:                 fd: arguments.rdi,
395:                 user_buffer: arguments.rsi,
396:                 length: arguments.rdx as usize,
397:             })
398:         }
399:         number::WAIT => Ok(SyscallRequest::Wait {
400:             child_pid: arguments.rdi,
401:             status_address: if arguments.rsi == 0 {
402:                 None
403:             } else {
404:                 Some(arguments.rsi)
405:             },
406:         }),
407:         number::SPAWN => {
408:             if arguments.rsi == 0 {
409:                 return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
410:             }
411:             if arguments.rsi > MAX_SPAWN_IMAGE_BYTES {
412:                 return Err(SyscallError::TooBig);
413:             }
414:             if arguments.rdx == 0 || arguments.rdx > MAX_SPAWN_STACK_PAGES {
415:                 return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
416:             }
417:             Ok(SyscallRequest::Spawn {
418:                 image_address: arguments.rdi,
419:                 image_length: arguments.rsi as usize,
420:                 stack_pages: arguments.rdx as usize,
421:             })
422:         }
423:         number::MMAP => {
424:             if arguments.rdi == 0 {
425:                 return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
426:             }
427:             if arguments.rdi > USER_ANONYMOUS_PAGE_BYTES {
428:                 return Err(SyscallError::TooBig);
429:             }
430:             Ok(SyscallRequest::Mmap {
431:                 length: arguments.rdi,
432:             })
433:         }
434:         number::MUNMAP => {
435:             if arguments.rsi != USER_ANONYMOUS_PAGE_BYTES
436:                 || arguments.rdi & (crate::paging::PAGE_SIZE - 1) != 0
437:             {
438:                 return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
439:             }
440:             Ok(SyscallRequest::Munmap {
441:                 address: arguments.rdi,
442:             })
443:         }
444:         number::MMAP_REGION => {
445:             if arguments.rdi == 0 {
446:                 return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
447:             }
448:             if arguments.rdi > MAX_USER_ANONYMOUS_REGION_BYTES {
449:                 return Err(SyscallError::TooBig);
450:             }
451:             Ok(SyscallRequest::MmapRegion {
452:                 length: arguments.rdi,
453:             })
454:         }
455:         number::MUNMAP_REGION => {
456:             if arguments.rsi > MAX_USER_ANONYMOUS_REGION_BYTES {
457:                 return Err(SyscallError::TooBig);
458:             }
459:             if arguments.rsi == 0
460:                 || arguments.rdi & (crate::paging::PAGE_SIZE - 1) != 0
461:                 || arguments.rsi & (crate::paging::PAGE_SIZE - 1) != 0
462:             {
463:                 return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
464:             }
465:             Ok(SyscallRequest::MunmapRegion {
466:                 address: arguments.rdi,
467:                 length: arguments.rsi,
468:             })
469:         }
470:         number::CLOSE => Ok(SyscallRequest::Close { fd: arguments.rdi }),
471:         number::DUP => Ok(SyscallRequest::Duplicate { fd: arguments.rdi }),
472:         _ => Err(SyscallError::NoSys),
473:     }
474: }

1: //! Fixed-capacity single-vCPU kernel-thread runtime and scheduling smoke tests.
2: //!
3: //! Thread stacks are allocator-owned 4 KiB mappings with one unmapped lower
4: //! guard page. Scheduler state is mutated only with local interrupts disabled.
5: //! The PIT handler may preempt a running thread by suspending its own call stack
6: //! on that thread's kernel stack and switching to another saved context.

56: /// Initialize the IRQ assembly's FXSAVE destination before any IRQ route is armed.
57: pub(crate) fn initialize_fpu_context() {
58:     // Bootstrap runs with IF clear; release stores the pointer before the timer
59:     // or device IRQ routes can be unmasked. IRQ assembly reads it with a plain
60:     // aligned 8-byte load, which is atomic on x86_64.
61:     ACTIVE_FX_STATE_POINTER.store(
62:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(BOOTSTRAP_FX_STATE),
63:         Ordering::Release,
64:     );
