70:         Self {
71:             offset_low: handler as u16,
72:             selector: KERNEL_CODE_SELECTOR,
73:             ist: ist & 0x07,
74:             attributes: 0x8e,
75:             offset_middle: (handler >> 16) as u16,
76:             offset_high: (handler >> 32) as u32,
77:             reserved: 0,
78:         }
79:     }
80: 
81:     fn user_interrupt_gate(handler: u64) -> Self {
82:         let mut entry = Self::interrupt_gate(handler, 0);
83:         entry.attributes = 0xee;
84:         entry
85:     }
86: }
87: 
88: #[repr(C, packed)]
89: struct TaskStateSegment {
90:     reserved0: u32,
91:     rsp0: u64,
92:     rsp1: u64,
93:     rsp2: u64,
94:     reserved1: u64,
95:     ist1: u64,
96:     ist2: u64,
97:     ist3: u64,
98:     ist4: u64,
99:     ist5: u64,
100:     ist6: u64,
101:     ist7: u64,
102:     reserved2: u64,
103:     reserved3: u16,
104:     iomap_base: u16,
105: }
106: 
107: impl TaskStateSegment {
108:     const EMPTY: Self = Self {
109:         reserved0: 0,
110:         rsp0: 0,
111:         rsp1: 0,
112:         rsp2: 0,
113:         reserved1: 0,
114:         ist1: 0,
115:         ist2: 0,
116:         ist3: 0,
117:         ist4: 0,
118:         ist5: 0,
119:         ist6: 0,
120:         ist7: 0,
121:         reserved2: 0,
122:         reserved3: 0,
123:         iomap_base: size_of::<Self>() as u16,
124:     };
125: }
126: 
127: #[repr(C, align(16))]
128: struct Stack([u8; STACK_SIZE]);
129: 
130: static mut GDT: [u64; 7] = [0; 7];
131: static mut IDT: IdtTable = IdtTable([IdtEntry::MISSING; 256]);
132: static mut TSS: TaskStateSegment = TaskStateSegment::EMPTY;
133: static mut RING0_STACK: Stack = Stack([0; STACK_SIZE]);
134: static mut DOUBLE_FAULT_STACK: Stack = Stack([0; STACK_SIZE]);
135: static mut IRQ_HANDLERS: [Option<IrqHandler>; 256] = [None; 256];
136: /// Whether a fatal exception diagnostic is in progress. A fault from inside
137: /// the fatal path stops silently instead of recursively printing.
138: static IN_FATAL_EXCEPTION: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
139: static USER_VIRTUAL_ADDRESS_BITS: AtomicU8 = AtomicU8::new(0);
140: 
141: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
142: pub(crate) type UserSyscallHandler =
143:     extern "C" fn(*mut rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallContext, *mut ()) -> i64;
144: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
145: pub(crate) type UserExceptionHandler = extern "C" fn(u64, *mut ()) -> bool;
146: 
147: #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
148: #[repr(C, align(16))]
149: struct UserKernelFxState([u8; 512]);
150: 
151: /// State retained by one suspended or running Ring 3 entry.
152: ///
153: /// The scheduler may later select among these records, so continuation, exit,
154: /// callback, and kernel FPU state must not be shared between processes.
155: #[repr(C, align(16))]
156: pub(crate) struct UserEntryState {
157:     resume_rsp: u64,
158:     exit_status: i32,
159:     exit_pending: u8,
160:     reserved: [u8; 3],
161:     previous: *mut UserEntryState,
162:     syscall_context: *mut (),
163:     kernel_fx_state: UserKernelFxState,
164: }
165: 
166: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
167: impl UserEntryState {
168:     pub(crate) const fn new() -> Self {
169:         Self {
170:             resume_rsp: 0,
171:             exit_status: 0,
172:             exit_pending: 0,
173:             reserved: [0; 3],
174:             previous: core::ptr::null_mut(),
175:             syscall_context: core::ptr::null_mut(),
176:             kernel_fx_state: UserKernelFxState([0; 512]),
177:         }
178:     }
179: }
180: 
181: const _: [(); 0] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(UserEntryState, resume_rsp)];
182: const _: [(); 8] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(UserEntryState, exit_status)];
183: const _: [(); 12] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(UserEntryState, exit_pending)];
184: const _: [(); 16] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(UserEntryState, previous)];
185: const _: [(); 24] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(UserEntryState, syscall_context)];
186: const _: [(); 32] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(UserEntryState, kernel_fx_state)];
187: 
188: #[no_mangle]
189: static mut RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY: *mut UserEntryState = core::ptr::null_mut();
190: 
191: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
192: #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
193: struct UserSyscallRuntime {
194:     handler: UserSyscallHandler,
195:     exception_handler: UserExceptionHandler,
196: }
197: 
198: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
199: static mut USER_SYSCALL_RUNTIME: Option<UserSyscallRuntime> = None;
200: 
201: /// A device interrupt callback. The callback is responsible for issuing EOI.
202: pub type IrqHandler = extern "C" fn();
203: 
204: /// Reasons an IRQ callback could not be registered.
205: #[allow(dead_code)]
206: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
207: pub enum IrqRegistrationError {
208:     InvalidVector,
209:     InterruptsEnabled,
210:     AlreadyRegistered,
211: }
212: 
213: // In 64-bit mode, exception entry saves SS:RSP even for a CPL 0 fault.
214: #[repr(C)]
215: // Register dumps are only compiled with the `fatal-register-dump` feature;
216: // the vector/error/RIP fields are always needed for the fatal diagnostic.
217: #[cfg_attr(not(feature = "fatal-register-dump"), allow(dead_code))]
218: pub(crate) struct ExceptionContext {
219:     r15: u64,
220:     r14: u64,
221:     r13: u64,
222:     r12: u64,
223:     r11: u64,
224:     r10: u64,
225:     r9: u64,
226:     r8: u64,
227:     rbp: u64,
228:     rdi: u64,
229:     rsi: u64,
230:     rdx: u64,
231:     rcx: u64,
232:     rbx: u64,
233:     rax: u64,
234:     vector: u64,
235:     error_code: u64,
236:     rip: u64,
237:     cs: u64,
238:     rflags: u64,
239:     rsp: u64,
240:     ss: u64,
241: }
242: 
243: const _: [(); 16] = [(); size_of::<IdtEntry>()];
244: const _: [(); 4096] = [(); size_of::<IdtTable>()];
245: const _: [(); 104] = [(); size_of::<TaskStateSegment>()];
246: const _: [(); 176] = [(); size_of::<ExceptionContext>()];
247: const _: [(); 120] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, vector)];
248: const _: [(); 128] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, error_code)];
249: const _: [(); 136] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, rip)];
250: const _: [(); 144] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, cs)];
251: const _: [(); 152] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, rflags)];
252: const _: [(); 160] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, rsp)];
253: const _: [(); 168] = [(); core::mem::offset_of!(ExceptionContext, ss)];
254: 
255: extern "C" {
256:     static exception_stub_table: [usize; EXCEPTION_COUNT];
257:     static irq_stub_table: [usize; IRQ_STUB_COUNT];
258:     fn rustix_load_gdt(descriptor_table_pointer: *const DescriptorTablePointer);
259:     fn rustix_syscall_stub();
260:     #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
261:     fn rustix_enter_user(
262:         frame: *const rustix_kernel::syscall::UserIretFrame,
263:         state: *mut UserEntryState,
264:     ) -> i32;
265:     #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
266:     fn rustix_user_exception_exit() -> !;
267: 
268: }
269: 
270: /// Install a private kernel GDT/TSS, exception gates, and device IRQ gates.
271: ///
272: /// The bootstrap runs with maskable interrupts disabled. This routine leaves
273: /// them disabled and does not modify CR3 or the inherited page-table hierarchy.
274: pub fn initialize() {
275:     // SAFETY: Single-core bootstrap ownership; the descriptor tables are private
276:     // static storage and are initialized before any gate is loaded.
277:     unsafe {
278:         asm!("cli", options(nomem, nostack));
279:         initialize_tss();
280:         initialize_gdt();
281:         initialize_idt();
282:     }
283: }
284: 
285: unsafe fn initialize_tss() {
286:     let ring0_stack = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(RING0_STACK).cast::<u8>();
287:     let double_fault_stack = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(DOUBLE_FAULT_STACK).cast::<u8>();
288:     // SAFETY: These pointers refer to the ends of distinct, aligned static stacks.
289:     let ring0_top = unsafe { ring0_stack.add(STACK_SIZE) } as u64;
290:     // SAFETY: These pointers refer to the ends of distinct, aligned static stacks.
291:     let double_fault_top = unsafe { double_fault_stack.add(STACK_SIZE) } as u64;
292:     let tss = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(TSS);
293: 
294:     // The architectural TSS fields are packed; use unaligned raw writes rather
295:     // than creating references to their potentially unaligned storage.
296:     unsafe {
297:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*tss).rsp0).write_unaligned(ring0_top);
298:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*tss).ist1).write_unaligned(double_fault_top);
299:     }
300: }
301: 
302: unsafe fn initialize_gdt() {
303:     let tss_base = core::ptr::addr_of!(TSS) as u64;
304:     let tss_limit = (size_of::<TaskStateSegment>() - 1) as u64;
305:     let low = (tss_limit & 0xffff)
306:         | ((tss_base & 0x00ff_ffff) << 16)
307:         | (TSS_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE << 40)
308:         | ((tss_limit & 0x000f_0000) << 32)
309:         | ((tss_base & 0xff00_0000) << 32);
310:     let high = tss_base >> 32;
311: 
312:     unsafe {
313:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[0]).write(0);
314:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[1]).write(0x00af_9a00_0000_ffff);
315:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[2]).write(0x00cf_9200_0000_ffff);
316:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[3]).write(low);
317:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[4]).write(high);
318:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[5]).write(USER_DATA_DESCRIPTOR);
319:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(GDT[6]).write(USER_CODE_DESCRIPTOR);
320:     }
321: 
322:     let gdtr = DescriptorTablePointer {
323:         limit: (size_of::<[u64; 7]>() - 1) as u16,
324:         base: core::ptr::addr_of!(GDT) as u64,
325:     };
326:     // SAFETY: `GDT` and its TSS descriptor are initialized and remain live.
327:     unsafe { rustix_load_gdt(&gdtr) };
328: }
329: 
330: unsafe fn initialize_idt() {
331:     let idt_entries = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(IDT.0).cast::<IdtEntry>();
332:     let stubs = core::ptr::addr_of!(exception_stub_table).cast::<usize>();
333: 
334:     for vector in 0..EXCEPTION_COUNT {
335:         // SAFETY: The assembly table contains exactly 32 stub addresses.
336:         let handler = unsafe { stubs.add(vector).read() } as u64;
337:         let ist = if vector == 8 { 1 } else { 0 };
338:         // SAFETY: `vector` is bounded by the statically allocated 256-entry IDT.
339:         unsafe {
340:             idt_entries
341:                 .add(vector)
342:                 .write(IdtEntry::interrupt_gate(handler, ist));
343:         }
344:     }
345: 
346:     let irq_stubs = core::ptr::addr_of!(irq_stub_table).cast::<usize>();
347:     for vector in IRQ_FIRST_VECTOR..=IRQ_LAST_VECTOR {
348:         // SAFETY: The assembly table contains one stub for each allocated vector.
349:         let handler = unsafe { irq_stubs.add(vector - IRQ_FIRST_VECTOR).read() } as u64;
350:         // SAFETY: `vector` is bounded by the statically allocated 256-entry IDT.
351:         unsafe {
352:             idt_entries
353:                 .add(vector)
354:                 .write(IdtEntry::interrupt_gate(handler, 0));
355:         }
356:     }
357:     // Vector 0x80 is reserved from device allocation and uses its own DPL-3
358:     // interrupt gate rather than the generic device-IRQ dispatch path.
359:     // SAFETY: The assembly defines this dedicated entry stub in the kernel image.
360:     unsafe {
361:         idt_entries
362:             .add(USER_SYSCALL_VECTOR)
363:             .write(IdtEntry::user_interrupt_gate(
364:                 rustix_syscall_stub as *const () as usize as u64,
365:             ));
366:     }
367: 
368:     // SAFETY: The final table entry is the dedicated 0xff spurious-IRQ stub.
369:     let spurious_handler =
370:         unsafe { irq_stubs.add(IRQ_VECTOR_COUNT + IRQ_UNWIRED_COUNT).read() } as u64;
371:     // SAFETY: The APIC spurious vector is a valid IDT index.
372:     unsafe {
373:         idt_entries
374:             .add(APIC_SPURIOUS_VECTOR)
375:             .write(IdtEntry::interrupt_gate(spurious_handler, 0));
376:     }
377:     // Vectors 0xf0-0xfe have no registered source; wire them to a controlled
378:     // fatal stop (through the shared dispatch, which halts) instead of leaving
379:     // non-present gates that would cascade into #GP with no diagnostics.
380:     for vector in IRQ_UNWIRED_FIRST_VECTOR..=IRQ_UNWIRED_LAST_VECTOR {
381:         // SAFETY: The assembly table provides one stub for each vector.
382:         let handler =
383:             unsafe { irq_stubs.add(IRQ_VECTOR_COUNT + (vector - IRQ_UNWIRED_FIRST_VECTOR)) }.read()
384:                 as u64;
385:         // SAFETY: `vector` is bounded by the statically allocated 256-entry IDT.
386:         unsafe {
387:             idt_entries
388:                 .add(vector)
389:                 .write(IdtEntry::interrupt_gate(handler, 0));
390:         }
391:     }
392: 
393:     let idtr = DescriptorTablePointer {
394:         limit: (size_of::<IdtTable>() - 1) as u16,
395:         base: core::ptr::addr_of!(IDT) as u64,
396:     };
397:     // SAFETY: Every architectural exception gate is initialized in static storage.
398:     unsafe { asm!("lidt [{}]", in(reg) &idtr, options(readonly, nostack, preserves_flags)) };
399: }
400: 
401: /// Configure the active user virtual-address width used to sanitize syscall returns.
402: ///
403: /// Call after boot CPU discovery and before any CPL3 code can execute.
404: pub fn configure_user_address_width(virtual_address_bits: u8) -> Result<(), KernelEntryStackError> {
405:     if !matches!(virtual_address_bits, 48 | 57) {
406:         return Err(KernelEntryStackError::InvalidVirtualAddressWidth);
407:     }
408:     USER_VIRTUAL_ADDRESS_BITS.store(virtual_address_bits, Ordering::Release);
409:     Ok(())
410: }
411: 
412: /// Errors returned while selecting the ring-0 entry stack for a user context.
413: #[allow(dead_code)]
414: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
415: pub enum KernelEntryStackError {
416:     InvalidVirtualAddressWidth,
417:     NonCanonical,
418:     NotKernelAddress,
419:     Misaligned,
420: }
421: 
422: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
423: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
424: pub enum UserRuntimeError {
425:     InterruptsEnabled,
426:     AlreadyInstalled,
427:     NotInstalled,
428:     InvalidEntryState,
429: }
430: 
431: /// Select the stack used by the next privilege transition from CPL3 to CPL0.
432: ///
433: /// # Safety
434: /// The TSS must already be initialized, local interrupts must be disabled, and
435: /// `stack_top` must be the aligned top of a live, writable supervisor stack that
436: /// is mapped in the active address space and every address space that may run
437: /// before this value is changed again. Callers must update it before entering a
438: /// different process. The function validates the address shape, not the mapping
439: /// or ownership of the stack memory.
440: #[allow(dead_code)]
441: pub unsafe fn set_kernel_entry_stack(
442:     stack_top: u64,
443:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
444: ) -> Result<(), KernelEntryStackError> {
445:     if !matches!(virtual_address_bits, 48 | 57) {
446:         return Err(KernelEntryStackError::InvalidVirtualAddressWidth);
447:     }
448:     if stack_top & 0xf != 0 {
449:         return Err(KernelEntryStackError::Misaligned);
450:     }
451:     if !rustix_kernel::memory::is_canonical_virtual_address(stack_top, virtual_address_bits) {
452:         return Err(KernelEntryStackError::NonCanonical);
453:     }
454:     if stack_top & (1u64 << (virtual_address_bits - 1)) == 0 {
455:         return Err(KernelEntryStackError::NotKernelAddress);
456:     }
457: 
458:     let tss = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(TSS);
459:     // SAFETY: The function contract requires initialized TSS storage; the field
460:     // is packed, so write without creating an aligned reference to it.
461:     unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*tss).rsp0).write_unaligned(stack_top) };
462:     Ok(())
463: }
464: 
465: /// Restore TSS.RSP0 to the bootstrap ring-0 stack.
466: ///
467: /// # Safety
468: /// The TSS must be initialized, local interrupts must be disabled, and the
469: /// caller must be executing on a different live stack before the restore.
470: #[allow(dead_code)]
471: pub unsafe fn restore_bootstrap_kernel_entry_stack(
472:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
473: ) -> Result<(), KernelEntryStackError> {
474:     let ring0_stack = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(RING0_STACK).cast::<u8>();
475:     // SAFETY: `RING0_STACK` is the initialized, fixed-size bootstrap entry stack.
476:     let ring0_top = unsafe { ring0_stack.add(STACK_SIZE) } as u64;
477:     // SAFETY: This function's contract establishes TSS initialization and IF=0.
478:     unsafe { set_kernel_entry_stack(ring0_top, virtual_address_bits) }
479: }
480: 
481: /// Install the syscall callback for one synchronously entered user process.
482: ///
483: /// # Safety
484: /// Local interrupts must be disabled, no other CPU may access this state, and
485: /// `context` must remain live until the callback is cleared after user return.
486: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
487: pub(crate) unsafe fn install_user_syscall_handler(
488:     handler: UserSyscallHandler,
489:     exception_handler: UserExceptionHandler,
490: ) -> Result<(), UserRuntimeError> {
491:     let flags: u64;
492:     // SAFETY: The temporary stack operation is balanced at CPL 0.
493:     unsafe { asm!("pushfq", "pop {}", out(reg) flags, options(preserves_flags)) };
494:     if flags & (1 << 9) != 0 {
495:         return Err(UserRuntimeError::InterruptsEnabled);
496:     }
497:     let runtime = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(USER_SYSCALL_RUNTIME);
498:     // SAFETY: IF is clear and the first implementation is single-vCPU.
499:     if unsafe { runtime.read() }.is_some() {
500:         return Err(UserRuntimeError::AlreadyInstalled);
501:     }
502:     // SAFETY: The caller keeps the function context live until clearing it.
503:     unsafe {
504:         runtime.write(Some(UserSyscallRuntime {
505:             handler,
506:             exception_handler,
507:         }))
508:     };
509:     // SAFETY: IF is clear and assembly reads this single-vCPU pointer only while
510:     // a Ring 3 entry is active.
511:     unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY).write(core::ptr::null_mut()) };
512:     Ok(())
513: }
514: 
515: /// Remove the user-process syscall callback after returning to the kernel root.
516: ///
517: /// # Safety
518: /// Local interrupts must be disabled, and no syscall callback may be in flight.
519: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
520: pub(crate) unsafe fn clear_user_syscall_handler() {
521:     // SAFETY: IF is clear and no user-entry trampoline may remain active when
522:     // clearing the callback or its process-local state.
523:     if !unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!(RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY).read() }.is_null() {
524:         fatal_irq_halt();
525:     }
526:     let runtime = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(USER_SYSCALL_RUNTIME);
527:     // SAFETY: The API contract guarantees single-vCPU exclusion with IF clear.
528:     unsafe { runtime.write(None) };
529: }
530: 
531: /// Request the syscall/exception assembly exit path after restoring the kernel root.
532: ///
533: /// # Safety
534: /// The kernel root must be active, the process root must no longer be active,
535: /// and execution must be on this single CPU with maskable interrupts disabled.
536: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
537: pub(crate) unsafe fn request_user_process_exit(status: i32) {
538:     // SAFETY: IF is clear and the active entry points to the live process state
539:     // whose syscall or exception callback is currently executing.
540:     let state = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!(RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY).read() };
541:     if state.is_null() {
542:         fatal_irq_halt();
543:     }
544:     unsafe {
545:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*state).exit_status).write(status);
546:         core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::Release);
547:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*state).exit_pending).write(1);
548:     }
549: }
550: 
551: /// Run a callback on a distinct live supervisor stack and return on the
552: /// original stack. The callback must return normally.
553: ///
554: /// # Safety
555: /// Enter a validated user frame and return only through the process-exit path.
556: ///
557: /// # Safety
558: /// The process root must already be active in CR3, TSS.RSP0 must point to a live
559: /// supervisor stack mapped in both roots, syscall and user-fault callbacks must
560: /// be installed, and maskable interrupts must be disabled.
561: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
562: pub(crate) unsafe fn enter_user(
563:     frame: rustix_kernel::syscall::ValidatedUserIretFrame,
564:     state: *mut UserEntryState,
565:     syscall_context: *mut (),
566: ) -> Result<i32, UserRuntimeError> {
567:     let flags: u64;
568:     // SAFETY: The temporary stack operation is balanced at CPL 0.
569:     unsafe { asm!("pushfq", "pop {}", out(reg) flags, options(preserves_flags)) };
570:     if flags & (1 << 9) != 0 {
571:         return Err(UserRuntimeError::InterruptsEnabled);
572:     }
573:     let runtime = core::ptr::addr_of!(USER_SYSCALL_RUNTIME);
574:     // SAFETY: IF is clear and only this CPU can update the runtime slot.
575:     if unsafe { runtime.read() }.is_none() {
576:         return Err(UserRuntimeError::NotInstalled);
577:     }
578:     if state.is_null() || (state as usize) & 0xf != 0 || syscall_context.is_null() {
579:         return Err(UserRuntimeError::InvalidEntryState);
580:     }
581:     // SAFETY: The caller provides one unique, aligned, live state record for
582:     // this user entry; it remains writable until the entry returns.
583:     unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*state).syscall_context).write(syscall_context) };
584:     let frame = frame.into_raw();
585:     // SAFETY: The caller establishes the root, stack, callback, and fault
586:     // policy invariants. The assembly returns only after this entry exits.
587:     Ok(unsafe { rustix_enter_user(&frame, state) })
588: }
589: 
590: /// Register one callback for an allocated device IRQ vector.
591: ///
592: /// # Safety
593: ///
594: /// The caller must keep the IRQ source masked while registering and ensure it
595: /// is masked whenever this callback is dispatched. The caller must serialize
596: /// registration against IRQ dispatch on every CPU. `handler` must not allocate,
597: /// format output, block, or unwind; it runs with IF disabled and owns the EOI.
598: /// The source must remain masked until all handler state is ready.
599: #[allow(dead_code)]
600: pub unsafe fn register_irq_handler(
601:     vector: u8,
602:     handler: IrqHandler,
603: ) -> Result<(), IrqRegistrationError> {
604:     let vector = vector as usize;
605:     if !(IRQ_FIRST_VECTOR..=IRQ_LAST_VECTOR).contains(&vector) {
606:         return Err(IrqRegistrationError::InvalidVector);
607:     }
608: 
609:     let flags: u64;
610:     // SAFETY: This reads RFLAGS at CPL 0; the temporary push/pop is balanced.
611:     unsafe {
612:         asm!("pushfq", "pop {}", out(reg) flags, options(preserves_flags));
613:     }
614:     if flags & (1 << 9) != 0 {
615:         return Err(IrqRegistrationError::InterruptsEnabled);
616:     }
617: 
618:     // SAFETY: The API contract requires registration to be serialized with IRQ
619:     // dispatch, and the vector has been checked against the table bounds.
620:     let slot = unsafe {
621:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(IRQ_HANDLERS)
622:             .cast::<Option<IrqHandler>>()
623:             .add(vector)
624:     };
625:     // SAFETY: `slot` points into the initialized static callback table.
626:     if unsafe { slot.read() }.is_some() {
627:         return Err(IrqRegistrationError::AlreadyRegistered);
628:     }
629:     // SAFETY: Registration is serialized by the caller's masking/IF contract.
630:     unsafe { slot.write(Some(handler)) };
631:     Ok(())
632: }
633: 
634: /// Device IRQ target called by the normalized assembly stubs.
635: #[no_mangle]
636: pub extern "C" fn rustix_irq_dispatch(vector: u64) {
637:     // SAFETY: guard records this entry frame for the heap's debug checks; it
638:     // decrements automatically on every return path.
639:     let _depth = InterruptContextGuard::enter();
640:     if vector == APIC_SPURIOUS_VECTOR as u64 {
641:         return;
642:     }
643: 
644:     if !(IRQ_FIRST_VECTOR as u64..=IRQ_LAST_VECTOR as u64).contains(&vector) {
645:         fatal_irq_halt();
646:     }
647: 
648:     // SAFETY: Interrupt gates clear IF, and registration's safety contract
649:     // serializes writes against this read. No mutable reference is created.
650:     let handler = unsafe {
651:         core::ptr::addr_of!(IRQ_HANDLERS)
652:             .cast::<Option<IrqHandler>>()
653:             .add(vector as usize)
654:             .read()
655:     };
656:     match handler {
657:         Some(handler) => handler(),
658:         None => fatal_irq_halt(),
659:     }
660: }
661: 
662: #[no_mangle]
663: pub extern "C" fn rustix_syscall_dispatch(context: *mut rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallContext) {
664:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::{
665:         sanitize_user_syscall_return_frame, SyscallArguments, UserIretFrame,
666:     };
667: 
668:     // SAFETY: The assembly entry is reached only through the DPL-3 syscall gate;
669:     // the stack pointer refers to its normalized GPR and hardware return frame.
670:     let _depth = InterruptContextGuard::enter();
671:     let Some(context) = (unsafe { context.as_mut() }) else {
672:         fatal_irq_halt();
673:     };
674:     let address_bits = USER_VIRTUAL_ADDRESS_BITS.load(Ordering::Acquire);
675:     if address_bits == 0 {
676:         fatal_irq_halt();
677:     }
678:     let frame = UserIretFrame {
679:         instruction_pointer: context.instruction_pointer,
680:         code_selector: context.code_selector,
681:         flags: context.flags,
682:         stack_pointer: context.stack_pointer,
683:         stack_selector: context.stack_selector,
684:     };
685:     let frame = match sanitize_user_syscall_return_frame(frame, address_bits) {
686:         Ok(frame) => frame.into_raw(),
687:         Err(_) => fatal_irq_halt(),
688:     };
689:     context.instruction_pointer = frame.instruction_pointer;
690:     context.code_selector = frame.code_selector;
691:     context.flags = frame.flags;
692:     context.stack_pointer = frame.stack_pointer;
693:     context.stack_selector = frame.stack_selector;
694: 
695:     let arguments = SyscallArguments {
696:         rdi: context.rdi,
697:         rsi: context.rsi,
698:         rdx: context.rdx,
699:         r10: context.r10,
700:         r8: context.r8,
701:         r9: context.r9,
702:     };
703:     #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
704:     let result = {
705:         let runtime = core::ptr::addr_of!(USER_SYSCALL_RUNTIME);
706:         // SAFETY: IF is clear and only this CPU can update the function table.
707:         let runtime = unsafe { runtime.read() };
708:         if let Some(runtime) = runtime {
709:             let state = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!(RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY).read() };
710:             if state.is_null() {
711:                 fatal_irq_halt();
712:             }
713:             // SAFETY: The active process state was installed by `enter_user` and
714:             // remains live on its kernel context while this syscall is handled.
715:             let callback_context = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*state).syscall_context).read() };
716:             (runtime.handler)(context, callback_context)
717:         } else {
718:             fallback_syscall_result(context.rax, arguments)
719:         }
720:     };
721:     #[cfg(not(feature = "probe-user-process"))]
722:     let result = fallback_syscall_result(context.rax, arguments);
723:     context.rax = result as u64;
724: }
725: 
726: fn fallback_syscall_result(
727:     number: u64,
728:     arguments: rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallArguments,
729: ) -> i64 {
730:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::{decode_syscall, SyscallError, SyscallRequest, ABI_VERSION};
731: 
732:     match decode_syscall(number, arguments) {
733:         Ok(SyscallRequest::AbiVersion) => ABI_VERSION as i64,
734:         Ok(
735:             SyscallRequest::Exit { .. }
736:             | SyscallRequest::Write { .. }
737:             | SyscallRequest::Wait { .. }
738:             | SyscallRequest::Spawn { .. }
739:             | SyscallRequest::Mmap { .. }
740:             | SyscallRequest::Munmap { .. }
741:             | SyscallRequest::MmapRegion { .. }
742:             | SyscallRequest::MunmapRegion { .. }
743:             | SyscallRequest::Close { .. }
744:             | SyscallRequest::Duplicate { .. },
745:         ) => SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value(),
746:         Err(error) => error.return_value(),
747:     }
748: }
749: 
750: fn fatal_irq_halt() -> ! {
751:     loop {
752:         // SAFETY: Unknown device interrupts are fatal and must not be retried.
753:         unsafe { asm!("cli; hlt", options(nomem, nostack)) };
754:     }
755: }
756: 
757: /// Fatal exception target called by the assembly stubs.
758: #[no_mangle]
759: pub extern "C" fn rustix_exception_dispatch(context: *const ExceptionContext) -> ! {
760:     // SAFETY: The common assembly stub passes a pointer to its normalized stack
761:     // frame, which remains live for the duration of this non-returning handler.
762:     let context = unsafe { &*context };
763:     #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
764:     if context.cs & 3 == 3 && !matches!(context.vector, 2 | 8 | 18) {
765:         let runtime = core::ptr::addr_of!(USER_SYSCALL_RUNTIME);
766:         // SAFETY: Exceptions arrive with IF clear; the diagnostic process runtime
767:         // is installed only for the synchronous single-vCPU user entry.
768:         if let Some(runtime) = unsafe { runtime.read() } {
769:             let state = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!(RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY).read() };
770:             if state.is_null() {
771:                 fatal_irq_halt();
772:             }
773:             // SAFETY: The active process entry remains live through its exception
774:             // callback and exit trampoline.
775:             let callback_context = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*state).syscall_context).read() };
776:             let depth = InterruptContextGuard::enter();
777:             crate::kprintln!(
778:                 "USER EXCEPTION: vector {} ({}) error={:#018x}; terminating process",
779:                 context.vector,
780:                 exception_name(context.vector),
781:                 context.error_code
782:             );
783:             if context.vector == 14 {
784:                 let fault_address: u64;
785:                 // SAFETY: Reading CR2 is privileged and this handler runs at CPL0.
786:                 unsafe {
787:                     asm!(
788:                         "mov {}, cr2",
789:                         out(reg) fault_address,
790:                         options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags)
791:                     )
792:                 };
793:                 crate::kprintln!("user page fault address (CR2): {:#018x}", fault_address);
794:             }
795:             if (runtime.exception_handler)(context.vector, callback_context) {
796:                 core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::Acquire);
797:                 // SAFETY: The callback's contract guarantees it has restored the
798:                 // kernel root and marked the process root inactive before setting
799:                 // this entry's exit flag.
800:                 if unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*state).exit_pending).read() } != 0 {
801:                     drop(depth);
802:                     // SAFETY: The exit callback restored CR3, completed teardown
803:                     // ownership transitions, and IF remains disabled. The asm
804:                     // trampoline restores the kernel FPU/stack continuation.
805:                     unsafe { rustix_user_exception_exit() };
806:                 }
807:             }
808:         }
809:     }
810: 
811:     // SAFETY: guard records this fatal entry frame; fatal paths never return.
812:     let _depth = InterruptContextGuard::enter();
813:     // A fault raised while this handler is running (including a #DF on the
814:     // IST stack) must not re-enter the printing path; stop silently instead.
815:     if IN_FATAL_EXCEPTION.swap(true, Ordering::Acquire) {
816:         loop {
817:             // SAFETY: Re-entrant fatal diagnostics are deliberately silent.
818:             unsafe { asm!("cli; hlt", options(nomem, nostack)) };
819:         }
820:     }
821:     crate::kprintln!(
822:         "FATAL EXCEPTION: vector {} ({}) error={:#018x} RIP={:#018x}",
823:         context.vector,
824:         exception_name(context.vector),
825:         context.error_code,
826:         context.rip
827:     );
828:     #[cfg(feature = "fatal-register-dump")]
829:     crate::kprintln!(
830:         "CS={:#06x} SS={:#06x} RFLAGS={:#018x} RSP={:#018x}",
831:         context.cs,
832:         context.ss,
833:         context.rflags,
834:         context.rsp
835:     );
836:     #[cfg(feature = "fatal-register-dump")]
837:     crate::kprintln!(
838:         "RAX={:#018x} RBX={:#018x} RCX={:#018x}",
839:         context.rax,
840:         context.rbx,
841:         context.rcx
842:     );
843:     #[cfg(feature = "fatal-register-dump")]
844:     crate::kprintln!(
845:         "RDX={:#018x} RSI={:#018x} RDI={:#018x} RBP={:#018x}",
846:         context.rdx,
847:         context.rsi,
848:         context.rdi,
849:         context.rbp
850:     );
851:     #[cfg(feature = "fatal-register-dump")]
852:     crate::kprintln!(
853:         "R8={:#018x} R9={:#018x} R10={:#018x} R11={:#018x}",
854:         context.r8,
855:         context.r9,
856:         context.r10,
857:         context.r11
858:     );
859:     #[cfg(feature = "fatal-register-dump")]
860:     crate::kprintln!(
861:         "R12={:#018x} R13={:#018x} R14={:#018x} R15={:#018x}",
862:         context.r12,
863:         context.r13,
864:         context.r14,
865:         context.r15
866:     );
867: 
868:     if context.vector == 14 {
869:         crate::kprintln!(
870:             "page fault: present={} write={} user={} reserved={} instruction-fetch={}",
871:             context.error_code & 1 != 0,
872:             context.error_code & 2 != 0,
873:             context.error_code & 4 != 0,
874:             context.error_code & 8 != 0,
875:             context.error_code & 16 != 0
876:         );
877:         let fault_address: u64;
878:         // SAFETY: Reading CR2 is a privileged x86_64 operation in this handler.
879:         unsafe {
880:             asm!("mov {}, cr2", out(reg) fault_address, options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags))
881:         };
882:         crate::kprintln!("page fault address (CR2): {:#018x}", fault_address);
883:     }
884: 
885:     loop {
886:         // SAFETY: Fatal exceptions are not recoverable in the current kernel.
887:         unsafe { asm!("cli; hlt", options(nomem, nostack)) };
888:     }
889: }
890: 
891: fn exception_name(vector: u64) -> &'static str {
892:     match vector {
893:         0 => "divide error",
894:         1 => "debug",
895:         2 => "non-maskable interrupt",
896:         3 => "breakpoint",
897:         4 => "overflow",
898:         5 => "bound range",
899:         6 => "invalid opcode",
900:         7 => "device not available",
901:         8 => "double fault",
902:         9 => "coprocessor segment overrun",
903:         10 => "invalid TSS",
904:         11 => "segment not present",
905:         12 => "stack-segment fault",
906:         13 => "general protection fault",
907:         14 => "page fault",
908:         16 => "x87 floating-point",
909:         17 => "alignment check",
910:         18 => "machine check",
911:         19 => "SIMD floating-point",
912:         20 => "virtualization",
913:         21 => "control protection",
914:         28 => "hypervisor injection",
915:         29 => "VMM communication",
916:         30 => "security exception",
917:         _ => "reserved or architecture-specific exception",
918:     }
919: }

922:     r#"
923:     .section .text
924:     .code64
925:

930:     push 0
931:     push \number
932:     jmp rustix_exception_common

935:     .macro RUSTIX_ISR_ERROR name, number

937:     .type \name, @function
938: \name:
939:     push \number
940:     jmp rustix_exception_common
941:     .endm

947:     RUSTIX_ISR_NO_ERROR rustix_exception_4, 4
948:     RUSTIX_ISR_NO_ERROR rustix_exception_5, 5

961:     RUSTIX_ISR_NO_ERROR rustix_exception_18, 18

976:     rustix_exception_common:
977:     cld
978:     push rax
979:     push rbx
980:     push rcx
981:     push rdx
982:     push rsi
983:     push rdi
984:     push rbp
985:     push r8
986:     push r9
987:     push r10
988:     push r11
989:     push r12
990:     push r13
991:     push r14

994:     and rsp, -16

998:     .global rustix_load_gdt
999:     .type rustix_load_gdt, @function
1000: rustix_load_gdt:
1001:     lgdt [rdi]
1002:     push 0x08
1003:     lea rax, [rip + 2f]
1004:     push rax
1005:     retfq
1006: 2:
1007:     mov ax, 0x10
1008:     mov ds, ax
1009:     mov es, ax
1010:     mov ss, ax
1011:     mov ax, 0x18
1012:     ltr ax
1013:     xor eax, eax
1014:     mov fs, ax
1015:     mov gs, ax
1016:     ret
1017: 
1018:     .section .rodata
1019:     .align 8
1020:     .global exception_stub_table
1021: exception_stub_table:
1022:     .quad rustix_exception_0, rustix_exception_1, rustix_exception_2, rustix_exception_3
1023:     .quad rustix_exception_4, rustix_exception_5, rustix_exception_6, rustix_exception_7
1024:     .quad rustix_exception_8, rustix_exception_9, rustix_exception_10, rustix_exception_11
1025:     .quad rustix_exception_12, rustix_exception_13, rustix_exception_14, rustix_exception_15
1026:     .quad rustix_exception_16, rustix_exception_17, rustix_exception_18, rustix_exception_19
1027:     .quad rustix_exception_20, rustix_exception_21, rustix_exception_22, rustix_exception_23
1028:     .quad rustix_exception_24, rustix_exception_25, rustix_exception_26, rustix_exception_27
1029:     .quad rustix_exception_28, rustix_exception_29, rustix_exception_30, rustix_exception_31
1030: 
1031:     .section .text
1032:     # Each IRQ entry is exactly 12 bytes: push synthetic error code, push its
1033:     # vector as imm32, then a rel32 jump to the shared GPR-saving entry.
1034:     .macro RUSTIX_IRQ_STUB vector
1035:     .byte 0x6a, 0
1036:     .byte 0x68
1037:     .long \vector
1038:     .byte 0xe9
1039:     .long rustix_irq_common - . - 4
1040:     .endm

1042:     .global rustix_irq_stub_start
1043: rustix_irq_stub_start:
1044:     .set irq_vector, 0x20
1045:     .rept 208
1046:     RUSTIX_IRQ_STUB irq_vector
1047:     .set irq_vector, irq_vector + 1
1048:     .endr
1049:     .set unwired_vector, 0xf0
1050:     .rept 15
1051:     RUSTIX_IRQ_STUB unwired_vector
1052:     .set unwired_vector, unwired_vector + 1
1053:     .endr
1054:     .global rustix_irq_spurious_stub
1055: rustix_irq_spurious_stub:
1056:     RUSTIX_IRQ_STUB 0xff

1058:     rustix_irq_common:
1059:     cld
1060:     push rax
1061:     push rbx
1062:     push rcx
1063:     push rdx
1064:     push rsi
1065:     push rdi
1066:     push rbp
1067:     push r8
1068:     push r9
1069:     push r10
1070:     push r11
1071:     push r12
1072:     push r13
1073:     push r14
1074:     push r15
1075:     # Preserve asynchronous x87/SSE state before Rust IRQ dispatch can use it.
1076:     mov rax, qword ptr [rip + rustix_active_fpu_state_pointer]
1077:     # A null pointer here means IRQ/FPU initialization order was broken; stop.
1078:     test rax, rax
1079:     jz rustix_irq_fatal_no_fx_state
1080:     fxsave64 [rax]
1081:     mov edi, dword ptr [rsp + 120]
1082:     mov rbx, rsp
1083:     and rsp, -16
1084:     call rustix_irq_dispatch
1085:     # A timer switch updates the active state pointer before selecting a context.
1086:     mov rax, qword ptr [rip + rustix_active_fpu_state_pointer]
1087:     test rax, rax
1088:     jz rustix_irq_fatal_no_fx_state
1089:     fxrstor64 [rax]
1090:     mov rsp, rbx
1091:     pop r15
1092:     pop r14
1093:     pop r13
1094:     pop r12
1095:     pop r11
1096:     pop r10
1097:     pop r9
1098:     pop r8
1099:     pop rbp
1100:     pop rdi
1101:     pop rsi
1102:     pop rdx
1103:     pop rcx
1104:     pop rbx
1105:     pop rax
1106:     add rsp, 16

1109:     .global rustix_syscall_stub
1110:     .type rustix_syscall_stub, @function
1111: rustix_syscall_stub:
1112:     cld
1113:     push rax
1114:     push rbx
1115:     push rcx
1116:     push rdx
1117:     push rsi
1118:     push rdi
1119:     push rbp
1120:     push r8
1121:     push r9
1122:     push r10
1123:     push r11
1124:     push r12
1125:     push r13
1126:     push r14
1127:     push r15
1128:     mov rbx, rsp
1129:     # A CPL0 software interrupt has no saved user RSP/SS; fail closed before
1130:     # passing such a frame to Rust as the complete syscall context.
1131:     mov rax, qword ptr [rbx + 128]
1132:     and eax, 3
1133:     cmp eax, 3
1134:     jne rustix_syscall_fatal_entry
1135:     # 15 pushed registers leave RSP 16-byte aligned from a TSS.RSP0 aligned top.
1136:     # Keep user x87/SSE state isolated from any Rust dispatch instructions.
1137:     sub rsp, 512
1138:     fxsave64 [rsp]
1139:     mov rdi, rbx
1140:     call rustix_syscall_dispatch
1141:     fxrstor64 [rsp]
1142:     mov rax, qword ptr [rip + RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY]
1143:     test rax, rax
1144:     jz .Lrustix_syscall_return_to_user
1145:     cmp byte ptr [rax + 12], 0
1146:     jne rustix_user_exit_return
1147: .Lrustix_syscall_return_to_user:
1148:     mov rsp, rbx
1149:     pop r15
1150:     pop r14
1151:     pop r13
1152:     pop r12
1153:     pop r11
1154:     pop r10
1155:     pop r9
1156:     pop r8
1157:     pop rbp
1158:     pop rdi
1159:     pop rsi
1160:     pop rdx
1161:     pop rcx
1162:     pop rbx
1163:     pop rax

1167:     .global rustix_enter_user
1168:     .type rustix_enter_user, @function
1169: rustix_enter_user:
1170:     # Preserve the SysV callee-saved kernel registers and remember the exact
1171:     # continuation stack before building the five-word privilege frame.
1172:     push rbx
1173:     push rbp
1174:     push r12
1175:     push r13
1176:     push r14
1177:     push r15
1178:     mov rbx, rdi
1179:     mov r12, rsi
1180:     test r12, r12
1181:     jz rustix_syscall_fatal_entry
1182:     mov qword ptr [r12], rsp
1183:     fxsave64 [r12 + 32]
1184:     mov rax, qword ptr [rip + RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY]
1185:     mov qword ptr [r12 + 16], rax
1186:     mov qword ptr [rip + RUSTIX_ACTIVE_USER_ENTRY], r12
1187:     push qword ptr [rbx + 32]
1188:     push qword ptr [rbx + 24]
1189:     push qword ptr [rbx + 16]
1190:     push qword ptr [rbx + 8]
1191:     push qword ptr [rbx]
1192:     xor eax, eax
1193:     xor ebx, ebx

2100: fn user_kernel_stack_top_for_slot(index: usize) -> Option<u64> {
2101:     if index >= rustix_kernel::process::MAX_PROCESSES {
2102:         return None;
2103:     }
2104:     // SAFETY: The index is within the fixed stack table. Each process slot has
2105:     // a distinct stack, and this single-vCPU probe runs one process at a time.
2106:     let stack = unsafe {
2107:         core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(USER_KERNEL_ENTRY_STACKS)
2108:             .cast::<UserKernelStack>()
2109:             .add(index)
2110:     };
2111:     // SAFETY: This raw address stays inside the selected static stack object.
2112:     let stack_start = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*stack).0).cast::<u8>() };
2113:     // SAFETY: Stack size is its exact byte extent and is page/alignment-multiple.
2114:     Some(unsafe { stack_start.add(USER_KERNEL_STACK_SIZE) } as u64)
2115: }

2117: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2118: fn process_kernel_entry_stack_top_for_slot<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
2119:     candidate: &rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTables<'_, M>,
2120:     index: usize,
2121: ) -> Option<u64> {
2122:     let entry_top = user_kernel_stack_top_for_slot(index)?;
2123:     let stack_start = entry_top.checked_sub(USER_KERNEL_STACK_SIZE as u64)?;
2124:     for address in (stack_start..entry_top).step_by(rustix_kernel::paging::PAGE_SIZE as usize) {
2125:         match candidate.walk(address) {
2126:             Ok(rustix_kernel::paging::PageWalkOutcome::Mapped(translation))
2127:                 if translation.permissions.writable
2128:                     && !translation.permissions.executable
2129:                     && !translation.permissions.user_accessible => {}
2130:             _ => return None,
2131:         }
2132:     }
2133:     Some(entry_top)
2134: }

2328: fn dispatch_user_spawn(
2329:     state_ptr: *mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
2330:     image_address: u64,
2331:     image_length: usize,
2332:     stack_pages: usize,
2333: ) -> i64 {
2334:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
2335: 
2336:     if image_length == 0
2337:         || image_length > rustix_kernel::syscall::MAX_SPAWN_IMAGE_BYTES as usize
2338:         || stack_pages == 0
2339:         || stack_pages > rustix_kernel::syscall::MAX_SPAWN_STACK_PAGES as usize
2340:         || image_address.checked_add(image_length as u64).is_none()
2341:     {
2342:         return SyscallError::InvalidArgument.return_value();
2343:     }
2344:     let Some(state) = (unsafe { state_ptr.as_ref() }) else {
2345:         return SyscallError::Io.return_value();
2346:     };
2347:     if state.process_id.get() != 1 {
2348:         return SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value();
2349:     }
2350:     let slots = state.child_context;
2351:     if slots.is_null() {
2352:         return SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value();
2353:     }
2354:     let mut slot = core::ptr::null_mut();
2355:     for index in 0..SPAWNED_CHILD_SLOTS {
2356:         let candidate = unsafe { slots.add(index) };
2357:         if !unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*candidate).ready).read() } {
2358:             slot = candidate;
2359:             break;
2360:         }
2361:     }
2362:     if slot.is_null() {
2363:         return SyscallError::OutOfMemory.return_value();
2364:     }
2365: 
2366:     let staging = core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(SPAWN_IMAGE_STAGING).cast::<u8>();
2367:     let roots_ptr =
2368:         state
2369:             .roots
2370:             .cast::<rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<
2371:                 '_,
2372:                 '_,
2373:                 '_,
2374:                 LimineHhdmCandidateMemory<'_>,
2375:             >>();
2376:     let user_memory_ptr = state.user_memory.cast::<LimineHhdmUserFrameMemory<'_>>();
2377:     let roots = unsafe { &*roots_ptr };
2378:     let Some(user_memory) = (unsafe { user_memory_ptr.as_mut() }) else {
2379:         return SyscallError::Io.return_value();
2380:     };
2381:     let mut copied = 0usize;
2382:     while copied < image_length {
2383:         let length = (rustix_kernel::paging::PAGE_SIZE as usize).min(image_length - copied);
2384:         let destination = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(staging.add(copied), length) };

2386:         // SAFETY: The destination is kernel-owned staging storage and cannot
2387:         // alias a frame mapped by the calling process. User bytes are copied
2388:         // only through checked frame-token access while the caller root is active.
2389:         match unsafe { roots.copy_from_user(state.process, source, destination, user_memory) } {
2390:             Ok(length) if length == destination.len() => copied += length,

2392:             Err(failure) => {
2393:                 return match failure.cause {
2394:                     rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessUserCopyError::Memory(_) => {
2395:                         SyscallError::Io.return_value()
2396:                     }
2397:                     _ => SyscallError::Fault.return_value(),
2398:                 };
2399:             }
2400:         }
2401:     }

2403:     let state = unsafe { &mut *state_ptr };
2404:     let roots = unsafe { &mut *roots_ptr };
2405:     switch_process_to_kernel_root(state, roots);
2406:     let image = unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(staging.cast_const(), image_length) };

2408:     switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);

2411:         Err(error) => error.return_value(),

2415: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2416: fn prepare_spawned_user_process<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
2417:     state: &mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
2418:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
2419:     slot: *mut SpawnedProcessTask,
2420:     image: &[u8],
2421:     stack_pages: usize,
2422: ) -> Result<u64, rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError> {
2423:     use rustix_kernel::paging::CachePolicy;
2424:     use rustix_kernel::process::UserProgramPrepareErrorKind;
2425:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
2426: 
2427:     let Some(allocator) = (unsafe { state.allocator.as_mut() }) else {
2428:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
2429:     };
2430:     let Some(user_memory) = (unsafe {
2431:         state
2432:             .user_memory
2433:             .cast::<LimineHhdmUserFrameMemory<'_>>()
2434:             .as_mut()
2435:     }) else {
2436:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
2437:     };
2438:     let Some(mtrr_layout) = (unsafe { state.mtrr_layout.as_ref() }) else {
2439:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
2440:     };
2441:     let mut select_cache = |mapping: rustix_kernel::paging::ValidatedMappingRange| {
2442:         mapping
2443:             .select_cache_attributes(
2444:                 CachePolicy::WriteBack,
2445:                 state.pat,
2446:                 mtrr_layout,
2447:                 state.normal_caching,
2448:             )
2449:             .map_err(|_| "no uniform write-back cache policy for spawned process page")
2450:     };
2451:     let stack_bytes = (stack_pages as u64)
2452:         .checked_mul(rustix_kernel::paging::PAGE_SIZE)
2453:         .ok_or(SyscallError::Overflow)?;
2454:     let lower_half_shift = u32::from(
2455:         state
2456:             .virtual_address_bits
2457:             .checked_sub(1)
2458:             .ok_or(SyscallError::InvalidArgument)?,
2459:     );
2460:     let stack_top = 1u64
2461:         .checked_shl(lower_half_shift)
2462:         .ok_or(SyscallError::InvalidArgument)?;
2463:     let stack_base = stack_top
2464:         .checked_sub(stack_bytes)
2465:         .ok_or(SyscallError::InvalidArgument)?;
2466:     let program = match rustix_kernel::process::prepare_user_program(
2467:         roots,
2468:         allocator,
2469:         user_memory,
2470:         image,
2471:         stack_base,
2472:         stack_pages as u64,
2473:         1,
2474:         state.physical_address_bits,
2475:         state.virtual_address_bits,
2476:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::USER_CODE_SELECTOR,
2477:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::USER_DATA_SELECTOR,
2478:         &mut select_cache,
2479:     ) {
2480:         Ok(program) => program,
2481:         Err(failure) => {
2482:             let error = match failure.kind {
2483:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::InvalidElf
2484:                 | UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::RegionReservation
2485:                 | UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackGuard
2486:                 | UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::InvalidInitialState
2487:                 | UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::InvalidIretFrame => SyscallError::InvalidArgument,
2488:                 UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::RootCreation
2489:                 | UserProgramPrepareErrorKind::StackAllocation => SyscallError::OutOfMemory,
2490:                 _ => SyscallError::Io,
2491:             };
2492:             if failure.cleanup(roots, allocator).is_err() {
2493:                 halt_forever();
2494:             }
2495:             return Err(error);

2599: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2600: fn rollback_unstarted_child(
2601:     process_table: &mut rustix_kernel::process::ProcessTable,
2602:     child_id: rustix_kernel::process::ProcessId,
2603: ) {
2604:     if process_table.exit(child_id, 127).is_err()
2605:         || !matches!(
2606:             process_table.try_wait(rustix_kernel::process::KERNEL_PROCESS_ID, child_id.get()),
2607:             Ok(rustix_kernel::process::WaitResult::Exited { pid, .. }) if pid == child_id
2608:         )
2609:     {
2610:         halt_forever();
2611:     }
2612: }

2614: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2615: fn destroy_spawned_child_root<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
2616:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
2617:     allocator: &mut rustix_kernel::frame::FrameAllocator,
2618:     slot: &mut SpawnedProcessTask,
2619: ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
2620:     if !slot.ready || !slot.root_owned {
2621:         return Ok(());
2622:     }
2623:     // SAFETY: A ready slot is initialized before the scheduler can dispatch it.
2624:     let process = unsafe { slot.context.assume_init_ref() }.process;
2625:     roots
2626:         .destroy(allocator, process)
2627:         .map_err(|_| "inactive spawned process root teardown failed")?;
2628:     slot.root_owned = false;
2629:     Ok(())
2630: }

2632: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2633: fn destroy_spawned_child_roots<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
2634:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
2635:     allocator: &mut rustix_kernel::frame::FrameAllocator,
2636:     slots: &mut [SpawnedProcessTask],
2637: ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
2638:     for slot in slots {
2639:         destroy_spawned_child_root(roots, allocator, slot)?;
2640:     }
2641:     Ok(())
2642: }

2644: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2645: fn dispatch_user_wait(
2646:     state_ptr: *mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
2647:     child_pid: u64,
2648:     status_address: Option<u64>,
2649: ) -> i64 {
2650:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
2651: 
2652:     // SAFETY: The installed callback context remains live while this scheduler
2653:     // task blocks and resumes; IF=0 serializes process-table access on one CPU.
2654:     let (
2655:         process_id,
2656:         process_table_ptr,
2657:         roots_ptr,
2658:         runtime_pointer,
2659:         entry_stack_top,
2660:         virtual_address_bits,
2661:         child_ptr,
2662:     ) = unsafe {
2663:         (
2664:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).process_id).read(),
2665:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).process_table).read(),
2666:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).roots)
2667:                 .read()
2668:                 .cast::<rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<
2669:                 '_,
2670:                 '_,
2671:                 '_,
2672:                 LimineHhdmCandidateMemory<'_>,
2673:             >>(),
2674:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).runtime_pointer).read(),
2675:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).kernel_entry_stack_top).read(),
2676:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).virtual_address_bits).read(),
2677:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).child_context).read(),
2678:         )
2679:     };
2680:     let result = loop {
2681:         let result = match unsafe { &*process_table_ptr }.peek_wait(process_id, child_pid) {
2682:             Ok(result) => result,
2683:             Err(rustix_kernel::process::ProcessTableError::NoChild) => {
2684:                 return SyscallError::NoChild.return_value();
2685:             }
2686:             Err(_) => return SyscallError::InvalidArgument.return_value(),
2687:         };
2688:         let rustix_kernel::process::WaitResult::Running { pid } = result else {
2689:             break result;
2690:         };
2691:         if child_ptr.is_null() {
2692:             return SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value();
2693:         }
2694:         let child_matches = (0..SPAWNED_CHILD_SLOTS).any(|index| {
2695:             let slot = unsafe { child_ptr.add(index) };
2696:             if !unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*slot).ready).read() } {
2697:                 return false;
2698:             }
2699:             let child_process = unsafe {
2700:                 core::ptr::addr_of!((*slot).context).cast::<ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext>()
2701:             };
2702:             unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*child_process).process_id).read() == pid }
2703:         });
2704:         if !child_matches {
2705:             return SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value();
2706:         }
2707: 
2708:         // Do not retain process-root borrows while the scheduler runs another
2709:         // task. The parent's syscall stack remains suspended on its entry stack.
2710:         let roots = unsafe { &mut *roots_ptr };
2711:         let state = unsafe { &*state_ptr };
2712:         switch_process_to_kernel_root(state, roots);
2713:         // SAFETY: The runtime belongs to this pinned scheduler task set; this
2714:         // syscall is IF-disabled and may suspend only the current task.
2715:         let woke = unsafe { crate::kernel_threads::wait_for_process_exit(runtime_pointer) };
2716:         if unsafe {
2717:             arch::x86_64::interrupts::set_kernel_entry_stack(entry_stack_top, virtual_address_bits)
2718:         }
2719:         .is_err()
2720:         {
2721:             halt_forever();
2722:         }
2723:         let roots = unsafe { &mut *roots_ptr };
2724:         let state = unsafe { &*state_ptr };
2725:         switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);
2726:         if !woke {
2727:             return SyscallError::Io.return_value();
2728:         }
2729:     };
2730: 
2731:     let rustix_kernel::process::WaitResult::Exited { pid, status } = result else {
2732:         return SyscallError::Io.return_value();
2733:     };
2734:     let (process, roots_ptr, user_memory_ptr, allocator_ptr) = unsafe {
2735:         (
2736:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).process).read(),
2737:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).roots)
2738:                 .read()
2739:                 .cast::<rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<
2740:                 '_,
2741:                 '_,
2742:                 '_,
2743:                 LimineHhdmCandidateMemory<'_>,
2744:             >>(),
2745:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).user_memory)
2746:                 .read()
2747:                 .cast::<LimineHhdmUserFrameMemory<'_>>(),
2748:             core::ptr::addr_of!((*state_ptr).allocator)
2749:                 .read()
2750:                 .cast::<rustix_kernel::frame::FrameAllocator>(),
2751:         )
2752:     };
2753:     if let Some(user_address) = status_address {
2754:         let status_bytes = status.to_le_bytes();
2755:         // SAFETY: Kernel-local status bytes are disjoint from allocator-owned
2756:         // user frames; the helper validates and copies through frame tokens.
2757:         match unsafe {
2758:             (&*roots_ptr).copy_to_user(process, user_address, &status_bytes, &mut *user_memory_ptr)
2759:         } {
2760:             Ok(4) => {}
2761:             Ok(_) => return SyscallError::Fault.return_value(),
2762:             Err(failure) => {
2763:                 return match failure.cause {
2764:                     rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessUserCopyError::Memory(_) => {
2765:                         SyscallError::Io.return_value()
2766:                     }
2767:                     _ => SyscallError::Fault.return_value(),
2768:                 };
2769:             }
2770:         }
2771:     }
2772: 
2773:     let child_slot = if child_ptr.is_null() {
2774:         return SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value();
2775:     } else {
2776:         (0..SPAWNED_CHILD_SLOTS).find_map(|index| {
2777:             let slot = unsafe { child_ptr.add(index) };
2778:             if !unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*slot).ready).read() } {
2779:                 return None;
2780:             }
2781:             let child_context = unsafe {
2782:                 core::ptr::addr_of!((*slot).context).cast::<ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext>()
2783:             };
2784:             (unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*child_context).process_id).read() } == pid)
2785:                 .then_some(slot)
2786:         })
2787:     };
2788:     let Some(child_slot) = child_slot else {
2789:         return SyscallError::NotSupported.return_value();
2790:     };
2791:     // Status copyout must succeed before releasing the child's address space.
2792:     // If teardown itself fails, the zombie and root ownership remain retryable.
2793:     let roots = unsafe { &mut *roots_ptr };
2794:     let allocator = unsafe { &mut *allocator_ptr };
2795:     let child_slot = unsafe { &mut *child_slot };
2796:     let root_was_owned = child_slot.root_owned;
2797:     if let Err(_) = destroy_spawned_child_root(roots, allocator, child_slot) {
2798:         return SyscallError::Io.return_value();
2799:     }
2800:     if root_was_owned {
2801:         kprintln!(
2802:             "spawned process root reclaimed after wait: pid={}",
2803:             pid.get()
2804:         );
2805:     }
2806:     match unsafe { &mut *process_table_ptr }.reap_exited(process_id, pid) {
2807:         Ok(rustix_kernel::process::WaitResult::Exited { pid: reaped, .. }) if reaped == pid => {
2808:             pid.get() as i64
2809:         }
2810:         _ => SyscallError::InvalidArgument.return_value(),
2811:     }
2812: }

2814: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2815: extern "C" fn run_user_process_task(runtime_pointer: *mut (), context: usize) {
2816:     let state_ptr = context as *mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext;
2817:     if state_ptr.is_null() || runtime_pointer.is_null() {
2818:         halt_forever();
2819:     }
2820:     // SAFETY: The process context belongs to this scheduler task and remains live
2821:     // until all tasks have exited. Each task installs its runtime pointer once.
2822:     unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*state_ptr).runtime_pointer).write(runtime_pointer) };
2823:     let (kernel_cr3, process_cr3, entry_stack_top, user_frame, virtual_address_bits, roots_ptr) = unsafe {
2824:         let state = &*state_ptr;
2825:         (
2826:             state.kernel_cr3,
2827:             state.process_cr3,
2828:             state.kernel_entry_stack_top,
2829:             state.user_frame,
2830:             state.virtual_address_bits,
2831:             state
2832:                 .roots
2833:                 .cast::<rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<
2834:                     '_,
2835:                     '_,
2836:                     '_,
2837:                     LimineHhdmCandidateMemory<'_>,
2838:                 >>(),
2839:         )
2840:     };
2841:     if arch::x86_64::cpu::read_cr3_raw() != kernel_cr3 {
2842:         halt_forever();
2843:     }
2844:     // SAFETY: Each PID has a distinct validated supervisor entry stack shared
2845:     // by the kernel and process roots. IF remains clear throughout user mode.
2846:     if unsafe {
2847:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::set_kernel_entry_stack(entry_stack_top, virtual_address_bits)
2848:     }
2849:     .is_err()
2850:     {
2851:         halt_forever();
2852:     }
2853:     let roots = unsafe { &mut *roots_ptr };
2854:     let state = unsafe { &*state_ptr };
2855:     switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);
2856:     if arch::x86_64::cpu::read_cr3_raw() != process_cr3 {
2857:         halt_forever();
2858:     }
2859:     let mut entry_state = arch::x86_64::interrupts::UserEntryState::new();
2860:     // SAFETY: This task owns its unique entry state and callback context until
2861:     // enter_user returns through the process's exit trampoline.
2862:     if unsafe {
2863:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::enter_user(
2864:             user_frame,
2865:             core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(entry_state),
2866:             state_ptr.cast(),
2867:         )
2868:     }
2869:     .is_err()
2870:     {
2871:         halt_forever();
2872:     }
2873:     if arch::x86_64::cpu::read_cr3_raw() != kernel_cr3 {
2874:         halt_forever();
2875:     }
2876:     // The task is now back on its scheduler-owned kernel stack; its process entry
2877:     // stack is no longer active. Restore the bootstrap TSS stack before exit.
2878:     if unsafe {
2879:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::restore_bootstrap_kernel_entry_stack(virtual_address_bits)
2880:     }
2881:     .is_err()
2882:     {
2883:         halt_forever();
2884:     }
2885: }

2887: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
2888: extern "C" fn run_deferred_user_process_task(runtime_pointer: *mut (), context: usize) {
2889:     let slot = context as *mut SpawnedProcessTask;
2890:     if slot.is_null() || runtime_pointer.is_null() {
2891:         halt_forever();
2892:     }
2893:     loop {
2894:         // SAFETY: The slot is initialized before the scheduler starts and remains
2895:         // on the outer probe stack. `ready` is published with IF disabled before
2896:         // the process-start queue wakes this task.
2897:         if unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!((*slot).ready).read() } {
2898:             let process_context = unsafe {
2899:                 core::ptr::addr_of_mut!((*slot).context).cast::<ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext>()
2900:             };
2901:             run_user_process_task(runtime_pointer, process_context as usize);
2902:             return;
2903:         }
2904:         // SAFETY: The scheduler owns this currently running cooperative task;
2905:         // the pinned runtime stays live until this entry exits.
2906:         if !unsafe { crate::kernel_threads::wait_for_process_start(runtime_pointer) } {
2907:             return;
2908:         }
2909:     }
2910: }
