3015:             halt_forever();
3016:         }
3017:     }
3018:     result.map(|()| region.start())
3019: }
3020: 
3021: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3022: fn unmap_anonymous_user_region<M, U>(
3023:     state: &mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
3024:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
3025:     user_memory: &mut U,
3026:     virtual_address: u64,
3027:     length: u64,
3028: ) -> Result<(), rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError>
3029: where
3030:     M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory,
3031:     U: rustix_kernel::paging::UserFrameMemory,
3032: {
3033:     use rustix_kernel::paging::PAGE_SIZE;
3034:     use rustix_kernel::process::UserRegionKind;
3035:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
3036: 
3037:     let Some(regions) = (unsafe { state.user_regions.as_mut() }) else {
3038:         halt_forever();
3039:     };
3040:     let exact_region = regions.regions().any(|region| {
3041:         region.start() == virtual_address
3042:             && region.length() == length
3043:             && region.kind() == UserRegionKind::Anonymous
3044:     });
3045:     if !exact_region {
3046:         return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
3047:     }
3048: 
3049:     let mut probe = [0u8; 1];
3050:     for offset in (0..length).step_by(PAGE_SIZE as usize) {
3051:         // SAFETY: The local byte is kernel stack storage; the helper accesses
3052:         // user frames only through allocator-issued frame tokens.
3053:         if let Err(failure) = unsafe {
3054:             roots.copy_from_user(
3055:                 state.process,
3056:                 virtual_address + offset,
3057:                 &mut probe,
3058:                 user_memory,
3059:             )
3060:         } {
3061:             return Err(match failure.cause {
3062:                 rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessUserCopyError::Memory(_) => SyscallError::Io,
3063:                 _ => SyscallError::Fault,
3064:             });
3065:         }
3066:     }
3067: 
3068:     switch_process_to_kernel_root(state, roots);
3069:     let Some(allocator) = (unsafe { state.allocator.as_mut() }) else {
3070:         halt_forever();
3071:     };
3072:     for page_index in (0..length / PAGE_SIZE).rev() {
3073:         let offset = page_index * PAGE_SIZE;
3074:         let frame = roots
3075:             .unmap_user_page(state.process, virtual_address + offset)
3076:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| halt_forever());
3077:         if allocator.deallocate(frame).is_err() {
3078:             halt_forever();
3079:         }
3080:     }
3081:     let Some(regions) = (unsafe { state.user_regions.as_mut() }) else {
3082:         halt_forever();
3083:     };
3084:     if regions.release_anonymous(virtual_address, length).is_err() {
3085:         halt_forever();
3086:     }
3087:     switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);
3088:     Ok(())
3089: }
3090: 
3091: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3092: fn allocate_and_map_user_page<M, U>(
3093:     state: &mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
3094:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
3095:     user_memory: &mut U,
3096:     virtual_address: u64,
3097: ) -> Result<(), rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError>
3098: where
3099:     M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory,
3100:     U: rustix_kernel::paging::UserFrameMemory,
3101: {
3102:     use rustix_kernel::paging::zero_user_frame;
3103:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
3104: 
3105:     let Some(allocator) = (unsafe { state.allocator.as_mut() }) else {
3106:         halt_forever();
3107:     };
3108:     let frame = allocator.allocate().ok_or(SyscallError::OutOfMemory)?;
3109:     if zero_user_frame(&frame, user_memory).is_err() {
3110:         if allocator.deallocate(frame).is_err() {
3111:             halt_forever();
3112:         }
3113:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
3114:     }
3115:     let (mapping, cache_attributes) = match user_mapping_attributes(state, virtual_address, &frame)
3116:     {
3117:         Ok(attributes) => attributes,
3118:         Err(error) => {
3119:             if allocator.deallocate(frame).is_err() {
3120:                 halt_forever();
3121:             }
3122:             return Err(error);
3123:         }
3124:     };
3125:     match roots.map_user_page(allocator, state.process, frame, mapping, cache_attributes) {
3126:         Ok(()) => Ok(()),
3127:         Err(failure) => {
3128:             let error = page_map_error_to_syscall(failure.cause);
3129:             if allocator.deallocate(failure.frame).is_err() {
3130:                 halt_forever();
3131:             }
3132:             Err(error)
3133:         }
3134:     }
3135: }
3136: 
3137: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3138: fn unmap_anonymous_user_page<M, U>(
3139:     state: &mut ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
3140:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
3141:     user_memory: &mut U,
3142:     virtual_address: u64,
3143: ) -> Result<(), rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError>
3144: where
3145:     M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory,
3146:     U: rustix_kernel::paging::UserFrameMemory,
3147: {
3148:     use rustix_kernel::paging::PAGE_SIZE;
3149:     use rustix_kernel::process::UserRegionKind;
3150:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
3151: 
3152:     let Some(regions) = (unsafe { state.user_regions.as_mut() }) else {
3153:         halt_forever();
3154:     };
3155:     let is_anonymous = regions.regions().any(|region| {
3156:         region.start() == virtual_address
3157:             && region.length() == PAGE_SIZE
3158:             && region.kind() == UserRegionKind::Anonymous
3159:     });
3160:     if !is_anonymous {
3161:         return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);
3162:     }
3163: 
3164:     let mut probe = [0u8; 1];
3165:     // SAFETY: This kernel-stack byte is disjoint from all allocator-owned user
3166:     // frames; copy_from_user validates and accesses the user frame by token.
3167:     if let Err(failure) =
3168:         unsafe { roots.copy_from_user(state.process, virtual_address, &mut probe, user_memory) }
3169:     {
3170:         return Err(match failure.cause {
3171:             rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessUserCopyError::Memory(_) => SyscallError::Io,
3172:             _ => SyscallError::Fault,
3173:         });
3174:     }
3175: 
3176:     switch_process_to_kernel_root(state, roots);
3177:     let Some(allocator) = (unsafe { state.allocator.as_mut() }) else {
3178:         halt_forever();
3179:     };
3180:     let frame = match roots.unmap_user_page(state.process, virtual_address) {
3181:         Ok(frame) => frame,
3182:         Err(_) => {
3183:             switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);
3184:             return Err(SyscallError::Io);
3185:         }
3186:     };
3187:     if let Err(failure) = allocator.deallocate(frame) {
3188:         let frame = failure.frame;
3189:         let (mapping, cache_attributes) =
3190:             match user_mapping_attributes(state, virtual_address, &frame) {
3191:                 Ok(attributes) => attributes,
3192:                 Err(_) => halt_forever(),
3193:             };
3194:         if let Err(failure) =
3195:             roots.map_user_page(allocator, state.process, frame, mapping, cache_attributes)
3196:         {
3197:             let _owned_frame = failure.frame;
3198:             halt_forever();
3199:         }
3200:         switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);
3201:         return Err(SyscallError::Io);
3202:     }
3203:     let Some(regions) = (unsafe { state.user_regions.as_mut() }) else {
3204:         halt_forever();
3205:     };
3206:     if regions
3207:         .release_anonymous(virtual_address, PAGE_SIZE)
3208:         .is_err()
3209:     {
3210:         halt_forever();
3211:     }
3212:     switch_kernel_to_process_root(state, roots);
3213:     Ok(())
3214: }
3215: 
3216: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3217: fn user_mapping_attributes(
3218:     state: &ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
3219:     virtual_address: u64,
3220:     frame: &rustix_kernel::frame::Frame,
3221: ) -> Result<
3222:     (
3223:         rustix_kernel::paging::ValidatedMappingRange,
3224:         rustix_kernel::paging::SelectedCacheAttributes,
3225:     ),
3226:     rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError,
3227: > {
3228:     use rustix_kernel::paging::{CachePolicy, PagePermissions, PAGE_SIZE};
3229:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
3230: 
3231:     let mapping = rustix_kernel::paging::validate_mapping_range(
3232:         virtual_address,
3233:         frame.address(),
3234:         PAGE_SIZE,
3235:         PagePermissions {
3236:             writable: true,
3237:             executable: false,
3238:             user_accessible: true,
3239:         },
3240:         Some(state.physical_address_bits),
3241:         state.virtual_address_bits,
3242:     )
3243:     .map_err(|_| SyscallError::InvalidArgument)?;
3244:     // SAFETY: The validated register snapshot lives in the synchronous process
3245:     // probe stack until the callback is cleared and the process returns.
3246:     let Some(mtrr_layout) = (unsafe { state.mtrr_layout.as_ref() }) else {
3247:         halt_forever();
3248:     };
3249:     let cache_attributes = mapping
3250:         .select_cache_attributes(
3251:             CachePolicy::WriteBack,
3252:             state.pat,
3253:             mtrr_layout,
3254:             state.normal_caching,
3255:         )
3256:         .map_err(|_| SyscallError::Io)?;
3257:     Ok((mapping, cache_attributes))
3258: }
3259: 
3260: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3261: fn switch_process_to_kernel_root<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
3262:     state: &ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
3263:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
3264: ) {
3265:     // SAFETY: Syscall entry has IF clear and runs on a stack mapped in both roots.
3266:     unsafe { arch::x86_64::cpu::write_cr3_with_global_flush(state.kernel_cr3) };
3267:     if arch::x86_64::cpu::read_cr3_raw() != state.kernel_cr3 {
3268:         halt_forever();
3269:     }
3270:     // SAFETY: The kernel root is active and no other CPU can use this process root.
3271:     if unsafe { roots.mark_inactive(state.process) }.is_err() {
3272:         halt_forever();
3273:     }
3274: }
3275: 
3276: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3277: fn switch_kernel_to_process_root<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
3278:     state: &ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext,
3279:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
3280: ) {
3281:     // SAFETY: The process root is owned and mapped for this synchronous run;
3282:     // the kernel/TSS stacks are shared and IF remains clear across the switch.
3283:     unsafe { arch::x86_64::cpu::write_cr3_with_global_flush(state.process_cr3) };
3284:     if arch::x86_64::cpu::read_cr3_raw() != state.process_cr3 {
3285:         halt_forever();
3286:     }
3287:     // SAFETY: CR3 now names this process root, and the shared kernel owner and
3288:     // root set remain live until this synchronous process exits.
3289:     if unsafe { roots.mark_active(state.process) }.is_err() {
3290:         halt_forever();
3291:     }
3292: }
3293: 
3294: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3295: fn user_region_error_to_syscall(
3296:     error: rustix_kernel::process::UserRegionError,
3297: ) -> rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError {
3298:     use rustix_kernel::process::UserRegionError;
3299:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
3300: 
3301:     match error {
3302:         UserRegionError::Capacity | UserRegionError::AnonymousAddressSpaceExhausted => {
3303:             SyscallError::OutOfMemory
3304:         }
3305:         UserRegionError::AddressOverflow => SyscallError::Overflow,
3306:         _ => SyscallError::InvalidArgument,
3307:     }
3308: }
3309: 
3310: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3311: fn page_map_error_to_syscall<E>(
3312:     error: rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessPageMapError<E>,
3313: ) -> rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError {
3314:     use rustix_kernel::paging::{CandidateMapErrorKind, ProcessPageMapError};
3315:     use rustix_kernel::syscall::SyscallError;
3316: 
3317:     match error {
3318:         ProcessPageMapError::Candidate(
3319:             CandidateMapErrorKind::OutOfFrames
3320:             | CandidateMapErrorKind::MappingCapacity
3321:             | CandidateMapErrorKind::TableCapacity,
3322:         ) => SyscallError::OutOfMemory,
3323:         ProcessPageMapError::Candidate(CandidateMapErrorKind::TableMemory(_)) => SyscallError::Io,
3324:         ProcessPageMapError::Candidate(CandidateMapErrorKind::Mapping(_)) => {
3325:             SyscallError::InvalidArgument
3326:         }
3327:         _ => SyscallError::InvalidArgument,
3328:     }
3329: }
3330: 
3331: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3332: extern "C" fn dispatch_active_user_process_exception(vector: u64, opaque: *mut ()) -> bool {
3333:     if vector >= 128 {
3334:         return false;
3335:     }
3336:     // SAFETY: `opaque` points to the same live, stack-owned process runtime used
3337:     // by syscall dispatch and remains valid until the exception exit trampoline.
3338:     let state = unsafe { &mut *opaque.cast::<ActiveUserProcessSyscallContext>() };
3339:     // SAFETY: The root manager is retained by the runtime for the synchronous
3340:     // user entry; this exception arrived on its mapped supervisor entry stack.
3341:     let roots = unsafe {
3342:         &mut *state
3343:             .roots
3344:             .cast::<rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<
3345:                 '_,
3346:                 '_,
3347:                 '_,
3348:                 LimineHhdmCandidateMemory<'_>,
3349:             >>()
3350:     };
3351:     finish_active_user_process(state, roots, 128 + vector as i32) == 0
3352: }
3353: 
3354: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3355: type PreparedUserProgram = rustix_kernel::process::PreparedUserProgram;
3356: 
3357: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3358: fn prepare_user_program<M, U, F>(
3359:     roots: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'_, '_, '_, M>,
3360:     allocator: &mut rustix_kernel::frame::FrameAllocator,
3361:     user_memory: &mut U,
3362:     image_bytes: &[u8],
3363:     stack_base: u64,
3364:     physical_address_bits: u8,
3365:     virtual_address_bits: u8,
3366:     select_cache: &mut F,
3367: ) -> Result<PreparedUserProgram, &'static str>
3368: where
3369:     M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory,
3370:     U: rustix_kernel::paging::UserFrameMemory,
3371:     F: FnMut(
3372:         rustix_kernel::paging::ValidatedMappingRange,
3373:     ) -> Result<rustix_kernel::paging::SelectedCacheAttributes, &'static str>,
3374: {
3375:     match rustix_kernel::process::prepare_user_program(
3376:         roots,
3377:         allocator,
3378:         user_memory,
3379:         image_bytes,
3380:         stack_base,
3381:         1,
3382:         1,
3383:         physical_address_bits,
3384:         virtual_address_bits,
3385:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::USER_CODE_SELECTOR,
3386:         arch::x86_64::interrupts::USER_DATA_SELECTOR,
3387:         select_cache,
3388:     ) {
3389:         Ok(program) => Ok(program),
3390:         Err(failure) => {
3391:             let message = failure.kind.message();
3392:             if let Err(_retained_resources) = failure.cleanup(roots, allocator) {
3393:                 halt_forever();
3394:             }
3395:             Err(message)
3396:         }
3397:     }
3398: }
3399: 
3400: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3401: fn probe_user_process_root<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
3402:     candidate: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTables<'_, M>,
3403:     memory_map: &rustix_kernel::memory::MemoryMap,
3404:     allocator: &mut rustix_kernel::frame::FrameAllocator,
3405:     cpu_info: &arch::x86_64::cpu::CpuInfo,
3406:     hhdm_offset: u64,
3407:     config: rustix_kernel::paging::PageWalkConfig,
3408: ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
3409:     let mut process_tasks = core::pin::pin!(crate::kernel_threads::new_process_tasks(
3410:         &BOOTSTRAP_TIMER_TICKS
3411:     )?);
3412:     crate::kernel_threads::prepare_process_task_stacks(
3413:         process_tasks.as_mut(),
3414:         candidate,
3415:         allocator,
3416:         cpu_info,
3417:         rustix_kernel::process::MAX_PROCESSES,
3418:     )?;
3419:     let result = probe_user_process_root_with_tasks(
3420:         candidate,
3421:         memory_map,
3422:         allocator,
3423:         cpu_info,
3424:         hhdm_offset,
3425:         config,
3426:         process_tasks.as_mut(),
3427:     );
3428:     crate::kernel_threads::reclaim_process_task_stacks(
3429:         process_tasks.as_mut(),
3430:         candidate,
3431:         allocator,
3432:     )?;
3433:     result
3434: }
3435: 
3436: #[cfg(feature = "probe-user-process")]
3437: fn probe_user_process_root_with_tasks<M: rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTableMemory>(
3438:     candidate: &mut rustix_kernel::paging::CandidatePageTables<'_, M>,
3439:     memory_map: &rustix_kernel::memory::MemoryMap,
3440:     allocator: &mut rustix_kernel::frame::FrameAllocator,
3441:     cpu_info: &arch::x86_64::cpu::CpuInfo,
3442:     hhdm_offset: u64,
3443:     config: rustix_kernel::paging::PageWalkConfig,
3444:     mut process_tasks: core::pin::Pin<&mut crate::kernel_threads::PreparedProcessTasks>,
3445: ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
3446:     use rustix_kernel::paging::{CachePolicy, MtrrState, PatLayout, ProcessAddressSpaceRoots};
3447: 
3448:     let process_setup_free_frames = allocator.free_frames();
3449:     let mut kernel_entry_stack_tops = [0; rustix_kernel::process::MAX_PROCESSES];
3450:     for (index, stack_top) in kernel_entry_stack_tops.iter_mut().enumerate() {
3451:         *stack_top = process_kernel_entry_stack_top_for_slot(candidate, index)
3452:             .ok_or("per-process kernel entry stack is not mapped RW/NX and supervisor-only")?;
3453:     }
3454:     let kernel_half = candidate
3455:         .share_supervisor_kernel_half()
3456:         .map_err(|_| "active kernel half could not be shared safely")?;
3457:     let physical_address_bits = cpu_info
3458:         .physical_address_bits
3459:         .ok_or("physical address width unavailable")?;
3460:     let pat = match cpu_info.pat_msr_value {
3461:         Some(value) => PatLayout::from_msr_value(value)
3462:             .map_err(|_| "PAT state is invalid for process mappings")?,
3463:         None => return Err("PAT state unavailable for process mappings"),
3464:     };
3465:     let mtrr_layout = match &cpu_info.mtrr {
3466:         MtrrState::Captured(registers) => registers
3467:             .validated_layout(physical_address_bits)
3468:             .map_err(|_| "MTRR state is invalid for process mappings")?,
3469:         _ => return Err("MTRR state unavailable for process mappings"),
3470:     };
3471:     let mut select_cache = |mapping: rustix_kernel::paging::ValidatedMappingRange| {
3472:         mapping
3473:             .select_cache_attributes(
3474:                 CachePolicy::WriteBack,
3475:                 pat,
3476:                 &mtrr_layout,
3477:                 cpu_info.normal_caching,
3478:             )
3479:             .map_err(|_| "no uniform write-back cache policy for process page")
3480:     };
3481:     let mut process_memory = LimineHhdmCandidateMemory {
3482:         memory_map,
3483:         hhdm_offset,
3484:         physical_address_bits,
3485:         virtual_address_bits: cpu_info.active_linear_address_bits,
3486:     };
3487:     let mut roots = match ProcessAddressSpaceRoots::new(&kernel_half, &mut process_memory, config) {
3488:         Ok(roots) => roots,
3489:         Err(failure) => {
3490:             if let Some(frame) = failure.frame {
3491:                 allocator
3492:                     .deallocate(frame)
3493:                     .map_err(|_| "process-root manager frame could not be reclaimed")?;
3494:             }
3495:             return Err("process-root manager creation failed");
3496:         }
3497:     };
3498: 
3499:     let mut user_memory = LimineHhdmUserFrameMemory {
3500:         memory_map,
3501:         hhdm_offset,
3502:         physical_address_bits: Some(physical_address_bits),
3503:         virtual_address_bits: cpu_info.active_linear_address_bits,
3504:     };
3505:     const USER_STACK_BASE: u64 = 0x0000_7fff_ffff_f000;
3506:     let init_program = match prepare_user_program(
3507:         &mut roots,
3508:         allocator,
3509:         &mut user_memory,
3510:         INIT_ELF_IMAGE,
3511:         USER_STACK_BASE,
3512:         physical_address_bits,
3513:         cpu_info.active_linear_address_bits,
3514:         &mut select_cache,
3515:     ) {
3516:         Ok(program) => program,
3517:         Err(error) => return Err(error),
3518:     };
3519:     let process = init_program.process;
3520:     let mut user_regions = init_program.regions;
3521:     let user_frame = init_program.user_frame;
3522: 
3523:     let mut process_table = rustix_kernel::process::ProcessTable::new();
3524:     let process_id = process_table
3525:         .create_initial()
3526:         .map_err(|_| "initial process record could not be created")?;
3527:     let process_kernel_stack_top = kernel_entry_stack_tops[0];
3528:     kprintln!(
3529:         "Ring 3 entry stack validated: pid={}, entry={:#x}",
3530:         process_id.get(),
3531:         process_kernel_stack_top
3532:     );
3533: 
3534:     let root_address = match roots.root_physical_address(process) {
3535:         Ok(address) => address,
3536:         Err(_) => {
3537:             roots
3538:                 .destroy(allocator, process)
3539:                 .map_err(|_| "process root could not be destroyed after root lookup failed")?;
3540:             return Err("process root address is unavailable");
3541:         }
3542:     };
3543:     let kernel_cr3 = arch::x86_64::cpu::read_cr3_raw();
3544:     let kernel_cr3_root = match rustix_kernel::paging::parse_cr3_root(
3545:         kernel_cr3,
3546:         Some(physical_address_bits),
3547:         cpu_info.pcid_enabled,
3548:     ) {
3549:         Ok(root) => root,
3550:         Err(_) => {
3551:             roots.destroy(allocator, process).map_err(|_| {
3552:                 "process root could not be destroyed after kernel CR3 validation failed"
3553:             })?;
3554:             return Err("active kernel CR3 could not be validated");
3555:         }
3556:     };
3557:     let process_cr3 = match rustix_kernel::paging::encode_cr3_value(
3558:         root_address,
3559:         kernel_cr3_root.low_bits(),
3560:         physical_address_bits,
3561:     ) {
3562:         Ok(value) => value,
3563:         Err(_) => {
3564:             roots

1972: /// Access to a complete allocator-owned user data frame.
1973: ///
1974: /// # Safety
1975: /// Implementations must access only the frame passed to each method and must
1976: /// bound reads/writes to the supplied offset and slice length. They must not
1977: /// create Rust references to user virtual addresses; this interface operates on
1978: /// kernel-owned frame tokens only.
1979: pub unsafe trait UserFrameMemory {
1980:     type Error;
1981: 
1982:     fn copy_from_frame(
1983:         &mut self,
1984:         frame: &Frame,
1985:         offset: usize,
1986:         destination: &mut [u8],
1987:     ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
1988: 
1989:     fn copy_to_frame(
1990:         &mut self,
1991:         frame: &Frame,
1992:         offset: usize,
1993:         source: &[u8],
1994:     ) -> Result<(), Self::Error>;
1995: }
1996: 
1997: /// Clear an allocator-owned user frame before exposing it to Ring 3.
1998: ///
1999: /// The writes are bounded to small chunks to avoid placing a full page on the
2000: /// kernel stack. On failure, the frame may be partially cleared and must not be
2001: /// mapped into a process.
2002: pub fn zero_user_frame<U: UserFrameMemory>(
2003:     frame: &Frame,
2004:     user_memory: &mut U,
2005: ) -> Result<(), U::Error> {
2006:     const ZERO_CHUNK: [u8; 128] = [0; 128];
2007: 
2008:     for offset in (0..PAGE_SIZE as usize).step_by(ZERO_CHUNK.len()) {
2009:         user_memory.copy_to_frame(frame, offset, &ZERO_CHUNK)?;
2010:     }
2011:     Ok(())
2012: }

2018:     executable: bool,

2028:     releasing: bool,
2029: }

2031: /// Fixed-capacity owner for roots that share a kernel upper half.
2032: ///
2033: /// All process roots in this set use one unique mutable memory-backend borrow;
2034: /// this avoids aliasing prepared-table handles across independent owners. The
2035: /// kernel root owner is borrowed separately through `SharedKernelHalf` and must
2036: /// outlive this set. User mappings are private, lower-half, allocator-owned 4 KiB
2037: /// leaves; mapping/unmapping is allowed only while a root is inactive. `destroy`
2038: /// returns private frames and tables but never releases shared kernel subtrees.
2039: /// This type does not switch CR3 or invalidate translations on behalf of callers.
2040: pub struct ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'share, 'kernel, 'memory, M: CandidatePageTableMemory> {
2041:     kernel_half: &'share SharedKernelHalf<'kernel>,
2042:     memory: &'memory mut M,
2043:     config: PageWalkConfig,
2044:     roots: [Option<ProcessRoot<M::PreparedTable>>; MAX_PROCESS_ADDRESS_SPACES],
2045:     generations: [u64; MAX_PROCESS_ADDRESS_SPACES],
2046: }
2047: 
2048: impl<'share, 'kernel, 'memory, M: CandidatePageTableMemory>
2049:     ProcessAddressSpaceRoots<'share, 'kernel, 'memory, M>
2050: {
2051:     pub fn new(
2052:         kernel_half: &'share SharedKernelHalf<'kernel>,
2053:         memory: &'memory mut M,
2054:         config: PageWalkConfig,
2055:     ) -> Result<Self, ProcessRootCreateFailure<M::Error>> {
2056:         if !candidate_config_is_valid(config) {

2058:                 cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::InvalidConfiguration,
2059:                 frame: None,
2060:             });
2061:         }
2062:         if config != kernel_half.config {
2063:             return Err(ProcessRootCreateFailure {
2064:                 cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::KernelConfigurationMismatch,
2065:                 frame: None,
2066:             });
2067:         }
2068:         Ok(Self {
2069:             kernel_half,
2070:             memory,
2071:             config,
2072:             roots: core::array::from_fn(|_| None),
2073:             generations: [0; MAX_PROCESS_ADDRESS_SPACES],
2074:         })
2075:     }
2076: 
2077:     pub const fn page_walk_config(&self) -> PageWalkConfig {
2078:         self.config
2079:     }
2080: 
2081:     /// Allocate a private root and copy only sanitized upper-half root entries.
2082:     pub fn create(
2083:         &mut self,
2084:         allocator: &mut FrameAllocator,
2085:     ) -> Result<ProcessAddressSpaceHandle, ProcessRootCreateFailure<M::Error>> {
2086:         let Some(slot) = self.roots.iter().position(Option::is_none) else {
2087:             return Err(ProcessRootCreateFailure {
2088:                 cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::Capacity,
2089:                 frame: None,
2090:             });
2091:         };
2092:         let generation =
2093:             self.generations[slot]
2094:                 .checked_add(1)
2095:                 .ok_or_else(|| ProcessRootCreateFailure {
2096:                     cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::GenerationExhausted,
2097:                     frame: None,
2098:                 })?;
2099:         let frame = allocator
2100:             .allocate()
2101:             .ok_or_else(|| ProcessRootCreateFailure {
2102:                 cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::OutOfFrames,
2103:                 frame: None,
2104:             })?;
2105:         let frame_end = frame.address().checked_add(PAGE_SIZE);
2106:         if !frame_end.is_some_and(|end| {
2107:             physical_range_fits_address_width(
2108:                 frame.address(),
2109:                 end,
2110:                 self.config.physical_address_bits,
2111:             )

2114:                 cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::RootFrameOutOfRange,
2115:                 frame: Some(frame),
2116:             });
2117:         }
2118:         let prepared = match self.memory.prepare_table(&frame) {
2119:             Ok(prepared) => prepared,
2120:             Err(error) => {
2121:                 return Err(ProcessRootCreateFailure {
2122:                     cause: ProcessRootCreateErrorKind::TableMemory(error),
2123:                     frame: Some(frame),
2124:                 });
2125:             }
2126:         };
2127:         // SAFETY: `prepared` was returned by this backend for the root token,
2128:         // which this root set retains until successful destruction.
2129:         unsafe { self.memory.clear_table(prepared) };

2134:                 // within the 512-entry top-level table.
2135:                 unsafe { self.memory.write_entry(prepared, index, entry) };

2138:         self.generations[slot] = generation;
2139:         self.roots[slot] = Some(ProcessRoot {
2140:             frame,
2141:             prepared,
2142:             tables: core::array::from_fn(|_| None),
2143:             mappings: core::array::from_fn(|_| None),
2144:             generation,
2145:             active: false,
2146:             releasing: false,
2147:         });
2148:         Ok(ProcessAddressSpaceHandle {
2149:             slot: slot as u8,
2150:             generation,
2151:         })
2152:     }

385:     #[cfg(rustix_user_fault_ud)]

396:             "mov rax, {guard}",
397:             "mov rax, qword ptr [rax]",
398:             guard = const USER_STACK_GUARD_ADDRESS,
399:             out("rax") _,
400:             options(nostack, readonly)
401:         );
402:     }
403: 
404:     #[cfg(rustix_user_fault_gp)]
405:     // SAFETY: DPL 0 vector 0x20 is intentionally invoked from Ring 3 to trigger

408:         asm!("int 0x20", options(nomem, nostack));
409:     }
410: 
411:     #[cfg(rustix_user_fault_de)]
412:     // SAFETY: The zero divisor deliberately produces user-mode #DE in QEMU.

414:         asm!(
415:             "xor edx, edx",
416:             "xor eax, eax",
417:             "xor ecx, ecx",
418:             "div ecx",
419:             out("eax") _,
420:             out("edx") _,
421:             out("ecx") _,
422:             options(nostack)
423:         );
424:     }
425: 
426:     loop {
427:         core::hint::spin_loop();
428:     }
429: }

56: /// Invoke one v1 syscall using the documented x86_64 register convention.
57: ///
58: /// This low-level entry is safe because pointer values are passed opaquely to
59: /// the kernel, which validates ranges and permissions before dereferencing.
60: /// Prefer [`write`], [`wait`], and [`exit`] when possible.
61: #[inline]
62: pub fn syscall(number: u64, arguments: [u64; 6]) -> i64 {
63:     let [first, second, third, fourth, fifth, sixth] = arguments;
64:     let result: u64;
65:     // SAFETY: The v1 ABI assigns RAX and the six argument registers exactly as
66:     // declared here. Rustix's DPL-3 gate and entry stub preserve Rust's memory
67:     // validity invariants by treating user pointers as integers in the kernel.
68:     unsafe {
69:         asm!(
70:             "int 0x80",
71:             inlateout("rax") number => result,
72:             in("rdi") first,
73:             in("rsi") second,
74:             in("rdx") third,
75:             in("r10") fourth,
76:             in("r8") fifth,
77:             in("r9") sixth,
78:             lateout("rcx") _,
79:             lateout("r11") _,
80:         );
81:     }
82:     result as i64
83: }
84: 
85: pub fn abi_version() -> Result<u64, SyscallError> {
86:     decode_result(syscall(SYS_ABI_VERSION, [0; 6]))
87: }
88: 
89: /// Write one buffer to a v1 file descriptor. A successful short write is valid.
90: pub fn write(file_descriptor: u64, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, SyscallError> {
91:     let result = decode_result(syscall(
92:         SYS_WRITE,
93:         [
94:             file_descriptor,
95:             bytes.as_ptr() as u64,
96:             bytes.len() as u64,
97:             0,
98:             0,
99:             0,
100:         ],
101:     ))?;
102:     usize::try_from(result).map_err(|_| SyscallError::ResultOutOfRange)
103: }
104: 
105: /// Close a process-local descriptor.
106: pub fn close(file_descriptor: u64) -> Result<(), SyscallError> {
107:     match decode_result(syscall(SYS_CLOSE, [file_descriptor, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]))? {
108:         0 => Ok(()),
109:         _ => Err(SyscallError::ResultOutOfRange),
110:     }
111: }

115:     decode_result(syscall(SYS_DUP, [file_descriptor, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]))
116: }

153: /// handle so safe code cannot accidentally release the same mapping twice.
154: pub struct MappedRegion {
155:     address: u64,
156: }

158: impl MappedRegion {

172: /// Map one zero-filled read/write, non-executable anonymous user page.
173: ///
174: /// `requested_length` must be in `1..=USER_PAGE_SIZE`; the kernel maps one page
175: /// and returns a `MappedRegion` whose length is exactly one page.
176: pub fn mmap(requested_length: usize) -> Result<MappedRegion, SyscallError> {
177:     let address = decode_result(syscall(SYS_MMAP, [requested_length as u64, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]))?;
178:     Ok(MappedRegion { address })
179: }

181: /// Unmap one page previously returned by [`mmap`].
182: pub fn munmap(region: MappedRegion) -> Result<(), SyscallError> {
183:     match decode_result(syscall(
184:         SYS_MUNMAP,
185:         [region.address, USER_PAGE_SIZE as u64, 0, 0, 0, 0],
186:     ))? {
187:         0 => Ok(()),
188:         _ => Err(SyscallError::ResultOutOfRange),
189:     }
190: }

212: /// Map a zero-filled read/write, non-executable anonymous region.
213: ///
214: /// The requested size is rounded up to a 4096-byte page. The current bounded
215: /// ABI supports regions up to [`MAX_MAPPED_REGION_SIZE`] bytes.
216: pub fn mmap_region(requested_length: usize) -> Result<MappedPages, SyscallError> {
217:     let address = decode_result(syscall(
218:         SYS_MMAP_REGION,
219:         [requested_length as u64, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
220:     ))?;
221:     let rounded_length = requested_length
222:         .checked_add(USER_PAGE_SIZE - 1)
223:         .ok_or(SyscallError::Overflow)?
224:         & !(USER_PAGE_SIZE - 1);
225:     Ok(MappedPages {
226:         address,
227:         length: rounded_length,
228:     })
229: }
230: 
231: /// Unmap an exact region returned by [`mmap_region`].
232: pub fn munmap_region(region: MappedPages) -> Result<(), SyscallError> {
233:     match decode_result(syscall(
234:         SYS_MUNMAP_REGION,
235:         [region.address, region.length as u64, 0, 0, 0, 0],
236:     ))? {
237:         0 => Ok(()),
238:         _ => Err(SyscallError::ResultOutOfRange),
239:     }

242: /// Write all bytes, retrying successful short writes.
243: ///
244: /// A zero-length write before the buffer is complete is reported as [`SyscallError::Io`]
245: /// to avoid an infinite loop.
246: pub fn write_all(file_descriptor: u64, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), SyscallError> {
247:     write_all_with(bytes, |remaining| write(file_descriptor, remaining))
248: }
249: 
250: /// Format directly to a file descriptor without allocating.
251: ///
252: /// Formatting stops at the first write failure and returns its syscall error.
253: pub fn write_fmt(
254:     file_descriptor: u64,
255:     arguments: core::fmt::Arguments<'_>,
256: ) -> Result<(), SyscallError> {
257:     write_fmt_with(arguments, |bytes| write(file_descriptor, bytes))
258: }
259: 
260: /// Best-effort panic reporting to stderr followed by process exit status 127.
261: pub fn panic_exit(info: &core::panic::PanicInfo<'_>) -> ! {
262:     let _ = write_fmt(STDERR, format_args!("rustix: panic: {info}\n"));
263:     exit(127)
264: }
265: 
266: fn write_all_with<W>(mut remaining: &[u8], mut write: W) -> Result<(), SyscallError>
267: where
268:     W: FnMut(&[u8]) -> Result<usize, SyscallError>,
269: {
270:     while !remaining.is_empty() {
271:         let written = write(remaining)?;
272:         if written == 0 {
273:             return Err(SyscallError::Io);
274:         }
275:         if written > remaining.len() {
276:             return Err(SyscallError::ResultOutOfRange);
277:         }
278:         remaining = &remaining[written..];
279:     }
280:     Ok(())
281: }
282: 
283: fn write_fmt_with<W>(arguments: core::fmt::Arguments<'_>, write: W) -> Result<(), SyscallError>
284: where
285:     W: FnMut(&[u8]) -> Result<usize, SyscallError>,
286: {
287:     let mut output = SyscallFormatter { write, error: None };
288:     match core::fmt::write(&mut output, arguments) {
289:         Ok(()) => Ok(()),
290:         Err(_) => Err(output.error.unwrap_or(SyscallError::Io)),
291:     }
292: }
293: 
294: struct SyscallFormatter<W> {
295:     write: W,
296:     error: Option<SyscallError>,
297: }
298: 
299: impl<W> core::fmt::Write for SyscallFormatter<W>
300: where
301:     W: FnMut(&[u8]) -> Result<usize, SyscallError>,
302: {
303:     fn write_str(&mut self, text: &str) -> core::fmt::Result {
304:         match write_all_with(text.as_bytes(), |remaining| (self.write)(remaining)) {
305:             Ok(()) => Ok(()),
306:             Err(error) => {
307:                 self.error = Some(error);
308:                 Err(core::fmt::Error)
309:             }
310:         }
311:     }
312: }

314: /// Wait for a child and optionally copy its signed 32-bit status to `status`.
315: ///
316: /// The current kernel probe supports already-exited children; a running child
317: /// returns [`SyscallError::NotSupported`] until blocking process scheduling is
318: /// integrated.
319: pub fn wait(child_pid: u64, status: Option<&mut i32>) -> Result<u64, SyscallError> {
320:     let status_address = status.map_or(0, |status| status as *mut i32 as u64);
321:     decode_result(syscall(SYS_WAIT, [child_pid, status_address, 0, 0, 0, 0]))
322: }
323: 
324: /// Exit the current process. The kernel ABI does not return from a successful exit.
325: pub fn exit(status: i32) -> ! {
326:     let _ = syscall(SYS_EXIT, [status as u32 as u64, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
327:     loop {
328:         core::hint::spin_loop();
329:     }
330: }
331: 
332: /// Run a process `main` and exit with its status.
333: ///
334: /// Applications define `_start` as a call to this helper so `main` can return
335: /// normally instead of managing the exit syscall itself.
336: pub fn run_main(main: fn() -> i32) -> ! {
337:     exit(main())
338: }

340: pub const HEAP_PAGE_SIZE: usize = USER_PAGE_SIZE;
341: const HEAP_MIN_BLOCK: usize = 16;
342: const HEAP_MAX_BLOCK: usize = 2048;
343: const HEAP_CLASS_COUNT: usize = 8;
344: 
345: /// Source of page-sized, page-aligned, zeroed read/write memory for the heap.
346: pub trait PageSource {
347:     fn allocate_page(&mut self) -> Option<NonNull<u8>>;
348: }
349: 
350: /// Page source backed by one `mmap` syscall per page. Pages are never returned.
351: pub struct MmapPages;
352: 
353: impl PageSource for MmapPages {
354:     fn allocate_page(&mut self) -> Option<NonNull<u8>> {
355:         let region = mmap(1).ok()?;
356:         if region.address() % HEAP_PAGE_SIZE as u64 != 0 {
357:             let _ = munmap(region);
358:             return None;
359:         }
360:         NonNull::new(region.as_mut_ptr())
361:     }
362: }
363: 
364: /// Power-of-two size-class allocator for requests up to 2048 bytes.
365: ///
366: /// Each class keeps an intrusive free list. Blocks are carved from whole pages,
367: /// so a block whose size is at least its alignment is naturally aligned. Freed
368: /// blocks are reused by their class; pages are never returned to the kernel.
369: /// Requests larger than 2048 bytes or requiring alignment above 2048 bytes return null.
370: pub struct SizeClassHeap<P> {
371:     pages: P,
372:     free: [Option<NonNull<FreeBlock>>; HEAP_CLASS_COUNT],
373: }
374: 
375: struct FreeBlock {
376:     next: Option<NonNull<FreeBlock>>,
377: }
378: 
379: impl<P: PageSource> SizeClassHeap<P> {
380:     pub const fn new(pages: P) -> Self {
381:         Self {
382:             pages,
383:             free: [None; HEAP_CLASS_COUNT],
384:         }
385:     }
386: 
387:     /// Return a block satisfying `layout`, or null when the request is out of
388:     /// range or no page can be obtained.
389:     pub fn allocate(&mut self, layout: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
390:         let Some(class) = size_class(layout) else {
391:             return null_mut();
392:         };
393:         if self.free[class].is_none() && !self.refill(class) {
394:             return null_mut();
395:         }
396:         let Some(block) = self.free[class] else {
397:             return null_mut();
398:         };
399:         // SAFETY: Every node on a free list was written by `refill` or
400:         // `deallocate` as an unused, exclusively owned block of this heap.
401:         self.free[class] = unsafe { block.as_ref().next };
402:         block.cast::<u8>().as_ptr()
403:     }
404: 
405:     /// Return a block to its size class.
406:     ///
407:     /// `pointer` must come from [`SizeClassHeap::allocate`] on this heap with the
408:     /// same `layout`, and must not be used afterward.
409:     pub fn deallocate(&mut self, pointer: NonNull<u8>, layout: Layout) {
410:         let Some(class) = size_class(layout) else {
411:             return;
412:         };
413:         let node = pointer.cast::<FreeBlock>();
414:         // SAFETY: The caller returns a block previously handed out for this
415:         // class, so it is at least `HEAP_MIN_BLOCK` bytes, suitably aligned,
416:         // and no longer in use.
417:         unsafe {
418:             node.as_ptr().write(FreeBlock {
419:                 next: self.free[class],
420:             });
421:         }
422:         self.free[class] = Some(node);
423:     }
424: 
425:     fn refill(&mut self, class: usize) -> bool {
426:         let Some(page) = self.pages.allocate_page() else {
427:             return false;
428:         };
429:         let block_size = HEAP_MIN_BLOCK << class;
430:         for index in (0..HEAP_PAGE_SIZE / block_size).rev() {
431:             // SAFETY: `page` is an owned, page-sized mapping and `index` keeps
432:             // `offset + block_size` within it. Page alignment and the power-of-two
433:             // block size make the node aligned for `FreeBlock`.
434:             let node = unsafe { page.as_ptr().add(index * block_size) }.cast::<FreeBlock>();
435:             // SAFETY: `node` is inside the page and is not yet visible to any user.
436:             unsafe {
437:                 node.write(FreeBlock {
438:                     next: self.free[class],
439:                 });
440:             }
441:             self.free[class] = NonNull::new(node);
442:         }
443:         true
444:     }
445: }
446: 
447: fn size_class(layout: Layout) -> Option<usize> {
448:     if layout.align() > HEAP_PAGE_SIZE {
449:         return None;
450:     }
451:     let block = layout
452:         .size()
453:         .max(layout.align())
454:         .max(HEAP_MIN_BLOCK)
455:         .checked_next_power_of_two()?;
456:     if block > HEAP_MAX_BLOCK {
457:         return None;
458:     }
459:     Some((block / HEAP_MIN_BLOCK).trailing_zeros() as usize)
460: }
461: 
462: /// Global allocator for Rustix user processes.
463: ///
464: /// Declare one with `#[global_allocator]` in an application that uses `alloc`.
465: /// Access is serialized by a spin lock, so the type may be shared safely.
466: pub struct UserAllocator {
467:     locked: AtomicBool,
468:     heap: UnsafeCell<SizeClassHeap<MmapPages>>,
469: }
470: 
471: // SAFETY: `heap` is only accessed inside `with_heap`, which holds `locked` for the
472: // full duration of the access, so there is never more than one live `&mut` to it.
473: unsafe impl Sync for UserAllocator {}
474: 
475: impl UserAllocator {
476:     pub const fn new() -> Self {
477:         Self {
478:             locked: AtomicBool::new(false),
479:             heap: UnsafeCell::new(SizeClassHeap::new(MmapPages)),
480:         }
481:     }
482: 
483:     fn with_heap<R>(&self, operation: impl FnOnce(&mut SizeClassHeap<MmapPages>) -> R) -> R {
484:         while self
485:             .locked
486:             .compare_exchange_weak(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed)
487:             .is_err()
488:         {
489:             core::hint::spin_loop();
490:         }
491:         // SAFETY: The acquired `locked` flag grants exclusive access until the
492:         // release store below, and the reference does not escape `operation`.
493:         let result = operation(unsafe { &mut *self.heap.get() });
494:         self.locked.store(false, Ordering::Release);
495:         result
496:     }
497: }
498: 
499: // SAFETY: Allocation and deallocation follow the `GlobalAlloc` contract: blocks
500: // returned by `alloc` satisfy `layout`, and `dealloc` is only given blocks from
501: // the same size class.
502: unsafe impl GlobalAlloc for UserAllocator {
503:     unsafe fn alloc(&self, layout: Layout) -> *mut u8 {
504:         self.with_heap(|heap| heap.allocate(layout))
505:     }
506: 
507:     unsafe fn dealloc(&self, pointer: *mut u8, layout: Layout) {
508:         if let Some(pointer) = NonNull::new(pointer) {
509:             self.with_heap(|heap| heap.deallocate(pointer, layout));
510:         }
511:     }
512: }
513: 
514: fn decode_result(result: i64) -> Result<u64, SyscallError> {
515:     if result >= 0 {
516:         return Ok(result as u64);
517:     }
518:     match result.checked_neg() {
519:         Some(1) => Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument),
520:         Some(2) => Err(SyscallError::BadFileDescriptor),
521:         Some(3) => Err(SyscallError::Fault),
522:         Some(4) => Err(SyscallError::OutOfMemory),
523:         Some(5) => Err(SyscallError::NoChild),
524:         Some(6) => Err(SyscallError::Interrupted),
525:         Some(7) => Err(SyscallError::Io),
526:         Some(8) => Err(SyscallError::NotImplemented),
527:         Some(9) => Err(SyscallError::ArgumentListTooLong),
528:         Some(10) => Err(SyscallError::Overflow),
529:         Some(11) => Err(SyscallError::NotSupported),
530:         Some(12) => Err(SyscallError::TooManyOpenFiles),
531:         _ => Err(SyscallError::Unknown(result)),
532:     }
533: }

535: #[cfg(test)]
536: mod heap_tests {
537:     use super::{PageSource, SizeClassHeap, HEAP_PAGE_SIZE};
538:     use core::alloc::Layout;
539:     use core::ptr::NonNull;
540: 
541:     #[repr(align(4096))]
542:     struct Page([u8; HEAP_PAGE_SIZE]);
543: 
544:     struct Arena<'a> {
545:         pages: &'a mut [Page],
546:         next: usize,
547:     }
548: 
549:     impl PageSource for Arena<'_> {
550:         fn allocate_page(&mut self) -> Option<NonNull<u8>> {
551:             let page = self.pages.get_mut(self.next)?;
552:             self.next += 1;
553:             NonNull::new(page.0.as_mut_ptr())
554:         }
555:     }
556: 
557:     fn arena(pages: &mut [Page]) -> SizeClassHeap<Arena<'_>> {
558:         SizeClassHeap::new(Arena { pages, next: 0 })
559:     }
560: 
561:     #[test]
562:     fn rounds_to_aligned_blocks_and_reuses_freed_blocks() {
563:         let mut storage: [Page; 2] = core::array::from_fn(|_| Page([0; HEAP_PAGE_SIZE]));
564:         let mut heap = arena(&mut storage);
565:         let layout = Layout::from_size_align(40, 8).unwrap();
566:         let first = heap.allocate(layout);
567:         assert!(!first.is_null());
568:         assert_eq!(first as usize % 64, 0);
569:         heap.deallocate(NonNull::new(first).unwrap(), layout);
570:         assert_eq!(heap.allocate(layout), first);
571:     }
572: 
573:     #[test]
574:     fn honors_large_alignment_within_a_page() {
575:         let mut storage: [Page; 1] = core::array::from_fn(|_| Page([0; HEAP_PAGE_SIZE]));
576:         let mut heap = arena(&mut storage);
577:         let layout = Layout::from_size_align(16, 1024).unwrap();
578:         let block = heap.allocate(layout);
579:         assert!(!block.is_null());
580:         assert_eq!(block as usize % 1024, 0);
581:     }
582: 
583:     #[test]
584:     fn rejects_oversized_overaligned_and_exhausted_requests() {
585:         let mut storage: [Page; 1] = core::array::from_fn(|_| Page([0; HEAP_PAGE_SIZE]));
586:         let mut heap = arena(&mut storage);
587:         assert!(heap
588:             .allocate(Layout::from_size_align(2049, 8).unwrap())
589:             .is_null());
590:         assert!(heap
591:             .allocate(Layout::from_size_align(8, 8192).unwrap())
592:             .is_null());
593:         // One page holds two 2048-byte blocks; the third needs a page the arena lacks.
594:         let max = Layout::from_size_align(2048, 8).unwrap();

I now have the full picture and a reviewed design. Let me record it, then implement incrementally.

**Design summary:**
1. **Identity/capacity**: PIDs stay strictly monotonic (never reused → ABA-safe user-visible identity); record slots, scheduler slots (generation-checked `ThreadId`), and root handles (generation-checked) are reused after reap. The PID-indexed entry-stack assumption (`pid-1`) is removed.
2. **Stack pools**: per task slot, a guarded scheduler stack (existing window `0xffff_c400…`) plus a guarded frame-backed TSS entry stack (new window `0xffff_c500…`). Table paths for all slots are pre-created before the kernel half is shared (shared PML4 entries snapshot at root creation; leaf-only changes below them stay visible to all roots). Stacks are mapped at spawn, unmapped/freed at task reap — never while a continuation lives on them.
3. **Child spawn**: any running process may spawn; tasks are created dynamically with context published before dispatch (IF=0 cooperative), replacing the 3 pre-reserved deferred slots.
4. **Wait/reap**: after status copyout + child-root destroy + record reap, the parent yields until the child's scheduler thread is `Exited`, then reclaims its task + both stacks.
5. **exec** (syscall 11, append-only, ABI minor bump): prepare replacement root/image/stack while old root inactive; on success switch CR3, destroy old root, rewrite the syscall return frame; on any failure restore the old root and return an error.
6. **Normal boot**: `boot-userspace` becomes a default feature; the same runtime runs during normal boot with report-and-fallback-to-shell failure policy; opt-in probes keep halt-on-failure diagnostics.

Each slot's stacks are mapped and then unmapped\n/// here; the candidate retains the intermediate tables, and later spawn/reap\n/// cycles only add or remove leaves inside those shared tables.\n#[cfg(feature = \"boot-userspace\")]\npub(crate) fn prepare_process_stack_tables<M: CandidatePageTableMemory>(\n    candidate: &mut CandidatePageTables<'_, M>,\n    allocator: &mut FrameAllocator,\n    cpu_info: &crate::arch::x86_64::cpu::CpuInfo,\n) -> Result<(), &'static str> {\n    let flags = disable_interrupts();\n    for slot in 0..MAX_THREADS {\n        for base in [THREAD_STACK_VIRTUAL_BASE, ENTRY_STACK_VIRTUAL_BASE] {\n            let stack_start = match prepare_stack_region(candidate, allocator, cpu_info, slot, base)\n            {\n                Ok(stack_start) => stack_start,\n                Err(error) => {\n                    restore_interrupts(flags);\n                    return Err(error);\n                }\n            };\n            rollback_stack(candidate, allocator, stack_start, THREAD_STACK_PAGES);\n        }\n    }\n    restore_interrupts(flags);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Create one process task with its scheduler stack and TSS entry stack.\n///\n/// The caller must publish the task's callback context before the current task\n/// next blocks, yields, or exits; with IF clear on this single vCPU no other\n/// task can dispatch in between. Returns the generation-checked thread identity\n/// and the top of its guarded syscall entry stack.\n#[cfg(feature = \"boot-userspace\")]\npub(crate) fn spawn_process_task<M: CandidatePageTableMemory>(\n    runtime: &mut KernelThreadRuntime,\n    candidate: &mut CandidatePageTables<'_, M>,\n    allocator: &mut FrameAllocator,\n    cpu_info: &crate::arch::x86_64::cpu::CpuInfo,\n    runtime_pointer: *mut KernelThreadRuntime,\n    entry: ThreadEntry,\n    argument: usize,\n) -> Result<(ThreadId, u64), &'static str> {\n    let slot = free_thread_slot(runtime)?;\n    let stack_start =\n        prepare_stack_region(candidate, allocator, cpu_info, slot, THREAD_STACK_VIRTUAL_BASE)?;\n    let entry_stack_start =\n        match prepare_stack_region(candidate, allocator, cpu_info, slot, ENTRY_STACK_VIRTUAL_BASE) {\n            Ok(stack_start) => stack_start,\n            Err(error) => {\n                rollback_stack(candidate, allocator, stack_start, THREAD_STACK_PAGES);\n                return Err(error);\n            }\n        };\n    let entry_stack_top = entry_stack_start + THREAD_STACK_PAGES as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;\n    let saved_rsp = match initialize_thread_stack(candidate, allocator, stack_start, slot, runtime_pointer)\n    {\n        Ok(saved_rsp) => saved_rsp,\n        Err(error) => {\n            rollback_stack(candidate, allocator, entry_stack_start, THREAD_STACK_PAGES);\n            rollback_stack(candidate, allocator, stack_start, THREAD_STACK_PAGES);\n            return Err(error);\n        }\n    };\n    let thread = match runtime.policy.create_thread() {\n        Ok(thread) if thread.slot_index() == slot => thread,\n        Ok(_) => {\n            rollback_stack(candidate, allocator, entry_stack_start, THREAD_STACK_PAGES);\n            rollback_stack(candidate, allocator, stack_start, THREAD_STACK_PAGES);\n            return Err(\"scheduler   "newString": "/// Expose the pinned runtime pointer for task creation and process helpers.\n///\n/// # Safety\n/// The caller must keep the returned runtime pinned and live until every\n/// process task exits, and obey the single-vCPU IF-disabled ownership contract.\n#[cfg(feature = \"boot-userspace\")]\npub(crate) unsafe fn process_runtime_pointer(\n    prepared: core::pin::Pin<&mut PreparedProcessTasks>,\n) -> *mut () {\n    // SAFETY: The caller maintains the pin; this only reads the stable address.\n    let prepared = unsafe { prepared.get_unchecked_mut() };\n    core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(prepared.runtime).cast::<()>()\n}\n\n/// Pre-create the page-table paths for every scheduler and entry stack slot.",

{
  "newString": "#[cfg(feature = \"boot-userspace\")]\nfn rollback_unstarted_child(\n    process_table: &mut rustix_kernel::process::ProcessTable,\n    parent: rustix_kernel::process::ProcessId,\n    child_id: rustix_kernel::process::ProcessId,\n) {\n    // The record was never published to a task; reap it through its real parent\n    // so the slot returns to the pool without leaving a zombie behind.\n    if process_table.exit(child_id, 127).is_err()\n        || !matches!(\n            process_table.try_wait(parent, child_id.get()),\n            Ok(rustix_kernel::process::WaitResult::Exited { pid, .. }) if pid == child_id\n        )\n    {\n        halt_forever();\n    }\n}",

  "newString": "/// Replace the calling process's image with a static ELF (syscall 11).\n///\n/// On success this call never returns: the process resumes Ring 3 at the\n/// replacement image's entry with a fresh zero-filled stack, keeping its PID,\n/// descriptors, parent, and children. The returned `Ok` value is uninhabited.\n/// On failure the original image continues and the error is returned. The\n/// kernel preserves the user x87/SSE state across a successful exec.\npub fn exec(image: &[u8], stack_pages: usize) -> Result<core::convert::Infallible, SyscallError> {\n    exec_with(image.as_ptr() as u64, image.len(), stack_pages, syscall)\n}\n\nfn exec_with(\n    image_address: u64,\n    image_length: usize,\n    stack_pages: usize,\n    invoke: impl FnOnce(u64, [u64; 6]) -> i64,\n) -> Result<core::convert::Infallible, SyscallError> {\n    if image_length == 0 || !(1..=MAX_INITIAL_STACK_PAGES).contains(&stack_pages) {\n        return Err(SyscallError::InvalidArgument);\n    }\n    if image_length > MAX_SPAWN_IMAGE_SIZE {\n        return Err(SyscallError::ArgumentListTooLong);\n    }\n    let result = invoke(\n        SYS_EXEC,\n        [\n            image_address,\n            image_length as u64,\n            stack_pages as u64,\n            0,\n            0,\n            0,\n        ],\n    );\n    // A successful exec replaces the process and never returns here.\n    Err(decode_result(result).unwrap_err())\n}\n\n/// A single page returned by the anonymous mapping syscall.",

1: # Unix-like process and syscall direction
2: 
3: **Status: bounded single-vCPU Milestone 4 exit criteria met locally on 2026-11-23; this does not claim a general-purpose userspace runtime.** The opt-in single-vCPU diagnostic runs a bundled Ring 3 init plus up to three children created through syscall 10 from user-supplied ELF bytes.
